# Ảnh chụp schema CSDL sau migrateToLatest (DB-MIG-001) — SINH và so khớp bởi packages/testkit/src/schema-drift.db.test.ts.
# Đọc từ pg_catalog (tên, không OID; sắp theo tên; chủ sở hữu chuẩn hoá <admin> = role migrate; chỉ Postgres 17). Schema đổi (migration mới) mà file này không đổi theo → test đỏ.
# Cập nhật có chủ đích SAU KHI xem diff và xác nhận đúng ý migration: pnpm db:snapshot

    WHEN role = 'platform_support'::text THEN support_reason IS NOT NULL AND char_length(btrim(support_reason)) >= 1 AND char_length(btrim(support_reason)) <= 500 AND expires_at <= (created_at + '72:00:00'::interval) AND property_ids = '{}'::uuid[] AND limits = '{}'::jsonb

END)

CASE
    WHEN (owner_type = 'identity_document'::text) THEN ((app.actor_role() = ANY (ARRAY['owner'::text, 'manager'::text, 'front_desk'::text])) AND app.actor_can_see_property(property_id))
    WHEN (owner_type = ANY (ARRAY['work_order'::text, 'handover'::text, 'meter_reading'::text, 'task'::text])) THEN true
    WHEN (owner_type = 'import_batch'::text) THEN ((app.actor_role() = ANY (ARRAY['owner'::text, 'manager'::text])) AND app.actor_can_see_property(property_id))
    WHEN (owner_type = 'residence_notice'::text) THEN ((app.actor_role() = ANY (ARRAY['owner'::text, 'manager'::text, 'front_desk'::text])) AND app.actor_can_see_property(property_id))
    WHEN (owner_type = 'payment_claim'::text) THEN (((app.actor_role() = ANY (ARRAY['owner'::text, 'manager'::text, 'accountant'::text, 'front_desk'::text])) AND app.actor_can_see_property(property_id)) OR ((app.actor_role() = 'tenant'::text) AND (uploaded_by = (NULLIF(current_setting('app.actor_id'::text, true), ''::text))::uuid)))
    WHEN (owner_type = 'export_job'::text) THEN (uploaded_by = (NULLIF(current_setting('app.actor_id'::text, true), ''::text))::uuid)
    WHEN (owner_type = 'lease_settlement'::text) THEN ((app.actor_role() = ANY (ARRAY['owner'::text, 'manager'::text, 'accountant'::text])) AND app.actor_can_see_property(property_id))
    ELSE false
END)) check ((organization_id = app.current_org()))

-- app.actor_can_see_property(p uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.actor_can_see_property(p uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM memberships m
      WHERE m.id = nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '')::uuid
        AND m.organization_id = nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid
        AND m.status = 'active'
        AND (m.valid_to IS NULL OR m.valid_to > now())
        AND (m.all_properties
             OR (p IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (
                   SELECT 1 FROM property_scopes ps
                   WHERE ps.organization_id = m.organization_id AND ps.membership_id = m.id AND ps.property_id = p))))
  $function$
-- app.actor_may_insert_person() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.actor_may_insert_person()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '') IS NULL
           OR coalesce(app.actor_role() IN ('owner','manager','front_desk'), false)
           OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memberships m WHERE m.id = nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '')::uuid)
  $function$
-- app.actor_may_update_person() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.actor_may_update_person()
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '') IS NULL
           OR coalesce(app.actor_role() IN ('owner','manager','front_desk','accountant'), false)
           OR NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memberships m WHERE m.id = nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '')::uuid)
  $function$

-- app.actor_person_id() owner=<admin>

 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'

    WHERE m.id = nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '')::uuid
      AND m.organization_id = nullif(current_setting('app.org_id', true), '')::uuid
      AND m.status = 'active'
      AND (m.valid_to IS NULL OR m.valid_to > now())
  $function$

-- app.allocation_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.allocation_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.reversed_at IS NOT NULL
     OR (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['reversed_at','reversal_entry_id']) <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['reversed_at','reversal_entry_id']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phân bổ %: chỉ được đảo một lần, không sửa nội dung', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.anonymize_identity_documents(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_months integer, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.anonymize_identity_documents(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_months integer, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_cut timestamptz := least(p_now, now()) - make_interval(months => greatest(p_months, 1));
  v_today date := (least(p_now, now()) AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date;
  v_ids uuid[];
  v_n integer;
BEGIN
  IF current_setting('transaction_isolation') <> 'read committed' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'app.anonymize_identity_documents requires READ COMMITTED (re-check after lock needs a fresh snapshot)';
  END IF;
  -- (1) pick + lock documents and their owners (FOR UPDATE conflicts with FK FOR KEY SHARE).
  SELECT coalesce(array_agg(c.id), '{}') INTO v_ids FROM (
    SELECT d.id FROM identity_documents d
    JOIN persons p ON p.organization_id = d.organization_id AND p.id = d.person_id
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL app.retention_person_activity(d.organization_id, d.person_id) a
    WHERE d.anonymized_at IS NULL
      AND d.updated_at < v_cut AND d.created_at < v_cut
      AND (d.retention_until IS NULL OR d.retention_until < v_today)
      AND NOT a.has_open
      AND (a.stay_lease_end IS NULL OR a.stay_lease_end < v_cut)
    ORDER BY d.updated_at, d.id
    LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 5000)
    FOR UPDATE OF d, p SKIP LOCKED
  ) c;
  IF cardinality(v_ids) = 0 THEN RETURN 0; END IF;
  -- (2) re-check with a fresh snapshot taken while holding the locks; same predicate as (1).
  WITH due AS (
    SELECT d.id, d.organization_id, d.file_id FROM identity_documents d
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL app.retention_person_activity(d.organization_id, d.person_id) a
    WHERE d.id = ANY (v_ids)
      AND d.anonymized_at IS NULL
      AND d.updated_at < v_cut AND d.created_at < v_cut
      AND (d.retention_until IS NULL OR d.retention_until < v_today)
      AND NOT a.has_open
      AND (a.stay_lease_end IS NULL OR a.stay_lease_end < v_cut)
  ), doc AS (
    UPDATE identity_documents d
       SET doc_number_enc = NULL, doc_number_key_id = NULL, doc_number_hash = NULL, doc_number_last4 = NULL,
           nationality = NULL, file_id = NULL, anonymized_at = now()
      FROM due WHERE d.id = due.id
    RETURNING d.id, d.organization_id, d.property_id, due.file_id
  ), f AS (
    UPDATE files f
       SET delete_after = least(coalesce(f.delete_after, (now() AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date), (now() AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date)
      FROM due
     WHERE f.organization_id = due.organization_id AND f.id = due.file_id AND f.status IN ('ready','pending_scan')
    RETURNING 1
  ), aud AS (
    INSERT INTO audit_events (id, organization_id, property_id, actor_type, action, entity_type, entity_id, after, reason,
                              correlation_id)
    SELECT gen_random_uuid(), doc.organization_id, doc.property_id, 'worker', 'identity_document.anonymized',
           'identity_document', doc.id,
           jsonb_build_object('retention_months', greatest(p_months, 1), 'file_scheduled_for_deletion', doc.file_id IS NOT NULL),
           'retention_expired', 'retention.privacy'
      FROM doc
    RETURNING 1
  )
  SELECT count(*)::int INTO v_n FROM doc;
  RETURN v_n;
END
$function$
-- app.anonymize_persons(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_months integer, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.anonymize_persons(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_months integer, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_cut timestamptz := least(p_now, now()) - make_interval(months => greatest(p_months, 1));
  v_ids uuid[];
  v_n integer;
BEGIN
  IF current_setting('transaction_isolation') <> 'read committed' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'app.anonymize_persons requires READ COMMITTED (re-check after lock needs a fresh snapshot)';
  END IF;
  -- (1) pick + lock persons (FOR UPDATE conflicts with FK FOR KEY SHARE, incl. new identity documents).
  SELECT coalesce(array_agg(c.id), '{}') INTO v_ids FROM (
    SELECT p.id FROM persons p
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL app.retention_person_activity(p.organization_id, p.id) a
    WHERE p.anonymized_at IS NULL
      AND p.updated_at < v_cut AND p.created_at < v_cut
      AND NOT a.has_open
      AND (a.relation_end IS NULL OR a.relation_end < v_cut)
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM identity_documents d
                      WHERE d.organization_id = p.organization_id AND d.person_id = p.id AND d.anonymized_at IS NULL)
    ORDER BY p.updated_at, p.id
    LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 5000)
    FOR UPDATE OF p SKIP LOCKED
  ) c;
  IF cardinality(v_ids) = 0 THEN RETURN 0; END IF;
  -- (2) re-check with a fresh snapshot taken while holding the locks; same predicate as (1).
  WITH upd AS (
    UPDATE persons p
       SET full_name = '***', phone = NULL, email = NULL, address = NULL, nationality = NULL, anonymized_at = now()
     WHERE p.id = ANY (v_ids)
       AND p.anonymized_at IS NULL
       AND p.updated_at < v_cut AND p.created_at < v_cut
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM app.retention_person_activity(p.organization_id, p.id) a
                       WHERE a.has_open OR a.relation_end >= v_cut)
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM identity_documents d
                       WHERE d.organization_id = p.organization_id AND d.person_id = p.id AND d.anonymized_at IS NULL)
    RETURNING p.id, p.organization_id
  ), aud AS (
    INSERT INTO audit_events (id, organization_id, actor_type, action, entity_type, entity_id, after, reason, correlation_id)
    SELECT gen_random_uuid(), upd.organization_id, 'worker', 'person.anonymized', 'person', upd.id,
           jsonb_build_object('retention_months', greatest(p_months, 1)), 'retention_expired', 'retention.privacy'
      FROM upd
    RETURNING 1
  )
  SELECT count(*)::int INTO v_n FROM upd;
  RETURN v_n;
END
$function$
-- app.approval_transition() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.approval_transition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status IN ('executed','rejected','cancelled')
     OR (OLD.status = 'approved' AND NEW.status NOT IN ('approved','executed'))
     OR NEW.requested_by <> OLD.requested_by OR NEW.kind <> OLD.kind
     OR NEW.amount IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.amount OR NEW.payload <> OLD.payload THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'approval_requests: không thể chuyển % → % hoặc sửa nội dung yêu cầu', OLD.status, NEW.status
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.audit_source_from_context() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.audit_source_from_context()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.source_ip IS NULL THEN NEW.source_ip := nullif(current_setting('app.source_ip', true), ''); END IF;
  IF NEW.user_agent IS NULL THEN NEW.user_agent := nullif(current_setting('app.user_agent', true), ''); END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.billing_period_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.billing_period_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE allowed text[];
BEGIN
  IF NEW.period_start <> OLD.period_start OR NEW.period_end <> OLD.period_end OR NEW.property_id <> OLD.property_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ thu %: không đổi khoảng ngày', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status <> OLD.status THEN
    allowed := CASE OLD.status
      WHEN 'draft' THEN ARRAY['reviewing']
      WHEN 'reviewing' THEN ARRAY['draft','issued']
      WHEN 'issued' THEN ARRAY['locked']
      WHEN 'locked' THEN ARRAY['issued']
      ELSE ARRAY[]::text[] END;
    IF NOT NEW.status = ANY (allowed) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ thu %: không thể chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status
        USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
    END IF;
  END IF;
  -- Phát hành theo thứ tự tháng (lượng tiêu thụ lùi về chỉ số đã duyệt gần nhất ⇒ ngược thứ tự là tính trùng).
  IF NEW.status = 'issued' AND OLD.status = 'reviewing' AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM billing_periods b
       WHERE b.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND b.property_id = NEW.property_id AND b.period_start < NEW.period_start
         AND b.status IN ('draft','reviewing')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ thu %: còn kỳ trước chưa phát hành', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.status IN ('issued','locked') AND (NEW.issued_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.issued_at OR NEW.issued_by IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.issued_by
       OR NEW.preview_hash IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.preview_hash) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ thu % đã phát hành', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'PERIOD_ISSUED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.billing_period_published(p_org uuid, p_id uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.billing_period_published(p_org uuid, p_id uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM billing_periods WHERE organization_id = p_org AND id = p_id AND status IN ('issued','locked'))
  $function$
-- app.business_date(p_org uuid, p_property uuid, p_at timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.business_date(p_org uuid, p_property uuid, p_at timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())
 RETURNS date
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
AS $function$
    SELECT ((p_at AT TIME ZONE p.timezone) - p.business_day_cutoff)::date
    FROM properties p WHERE p.organization_id = p_org AND p.id = p_property
  $function$
-- app.charge_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.charge_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM invoice_lines il WHERE il.organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND il.charge_id = OLD.id)
     OR (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['voided_at','voided_by','void_reason']) <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['voided_at','voided_by','void_reason']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'khoản phải thu % đã lên phiếu hoặc chỉ được huỷ', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN RETURN OLD; END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.check_allocation_by() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_allocation_by()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.allocated_by IS NULL AND NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM payments p WHERE p.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND p.id = NEW.payment_id
         AND p.source = 'bank_auto') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phân bổ % thiếu người lập', NEW.id USING ERRCODE = 'not_null_violation';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.check_bank_auto_payment() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_bank_auto_payment()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.source = 'bank_auto' AND NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM inbox i
       WHERE i.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND i.bank_transaction_id = NEW.bank_transaction_id
         AND i.signature_verified AND i.provider <> 'statement_csv') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'thanh toán tự động % thiếu webhook đã xác minh', NEW.id
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'EVIDENCE_REQUIRED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.check_billing_period_lock() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_billing_period_lock()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE lt date := app.locked_through(NEW.organization_id, NEW.property_id);
BEGIN
  IF NEW.status = 'locked' AND NOT (lt IS NOT NULL AND lt >= NEW.period_end) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ thu % khoá nhưng sổ chưa khoá tới %', NEW.id, NEW.period_end
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status IN ('issued','draft','reviewing') AND lt IS NOT NULL AND lt >= NEW.period_end THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ thu % nằm trong khoảng sổ đã khoá nhưng không ở trạng thái locked', NEW.id
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $function$
-- app.check_block_allocation() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_block_allocation()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE n integer;
BEGIN
  SELECT count(*) INTO n FROM allocations
   WHERE organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND kind = 'block' AND source_id = NEW.id AND active;
  IF NEW.status = 'approved' AND n <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'block % approved nhưng không có đúng một allocation active', NEW.id USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status IN ('ended','rejected','requested') AND n <> 0 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'block % ở trạng thái % nhưng allocation vẫn active', NEW.id, NEW.status USING ERRCODE = '23514';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $function$
-- app.check_entry_balanced() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_entry_balanced()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE d bigint; c bigint; n integer; e uuid;
BEGIN
  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'journal_lines' THEN e := NEW.entry_id; ELSE e := NEW.id; END IF;
  SELECT COALESCE(sum(debit), 0), COALESCE(sum(credit), 0), count(*) INTO d, c, n FROM journal_lines WHERE entry_id = e;
  IF n < 2 OR d <> c THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'bút toán % lệch hoặc thiếu dòng (Nợ %, Có %, % dòng)', e, d, c, n
      USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'LEDGER_UNBALANCED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $function$
-- app.check_invoice_total() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_invoice_total()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE v_total bigint; v_sum bigint; v_n int; v_id uuid; v_org uuid;
BEGIN
  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'invoices' THEN v_id := NEW.id; ELSE v_id := NEW.invoice_id; END IF;
  v_org := NEW.organization_id;
  SELECT total INTO v_total FROM invoices WHERE organization_id = v_org AND id = v_id;
  SELECT coalesce(sum(amount), 0), count(*) INTO v_sum, v_n FROM invoice_lines WHERE organization_id = v_org AND invoice_id = v_id;
  IF v_n = 0 OR v_sum <> v_total THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiếu %: tổng % ≠ Σ dòng % (% dòng)', v_id, v_total, v_sum, v_n
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'VALIDATION_FAILED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $function$
-- app.check_last_owner() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_last_owner()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE org uuid := COALESCE(NEW.organization_id, OLD.organization_id);
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memberships WHERE organization_id = org AND role = 'owner' AND status = 'active') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'tổ chức % không còn owner active', org USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'LAST_OWNER';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $function$
-- app.check_lease_representative() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_lease_representative()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_lease uuid; v_org uuid; v_status text; v_rep uuid; v_n int; v_match int;
BEGIN
  v_org := NEW.organization_id;
  -- IF (không dùng CASE): plpgsql phân giải mọi trường trong biểu thức, leases không có lease_id.
  IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'leases' THEN v_lease := NEW.id; ELSE v_lease := NEW.lease_id; END IF;
  SELECT status, representative_person_id INTO v_status, v_rep FROM leases WHERE organization_id = v_org AND id = v_lease;
  IF v_status IN ('closed','cancelled') THEN RETURN NULL; END IF;
  SELECT count(*), count(*) FILTER (WHERE person_id = v_rep) INTO v_n, v_match FROM lease_occupants
  WHERE organization_id = v_org AND lease_id = v_lease AND is_representative AND to_on IS NULL;
  IF v_n <> 1 OR v_match <> 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hợp đồng %: cần đúng một người đại diện đang ở, trùng người đứng hợp đồng', v_lease
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'VALIDATION_FAILED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $function$
-- app.check_period_open() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_period_open()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE
  lt date := app.locked_through(NEW.organization_id, NEW.property_id);
BEGIN
  IF lt IS NOT NULL AND NEW.business_date <= lt THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ đã khoá tới % (ngày kinh doanh %)', lt, NEW.business_date
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'PERIOD_LOCKED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.check_property_not_in_use() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_property_not_in_use()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  PERFORM 1 FROM properties WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND id = OLD.id FOR UPDATE;
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM allocations WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM reservations WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM stays WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM leases WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM invoices WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM payments WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM deposits WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM journal_entries WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM shifts WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM billing_periods WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM meter_readings WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
     OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM bank_transactions WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND property_id = OLD.id)
  THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'cơ sở % đã có giao dịch — không đổi múi giờ hoặc giờ chốt ngày', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'PROPERTY_IN_USE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.check_timezone() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.check_timezone()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM pg_timezone_names WHERE name = NEW.timezone) OR NEW.timezone !~ '/' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'timezone không hợp lệ: %', NEW.timezone USING ERRCODE = '22023', HINT = 'VALIDATION_FAILED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.claim_due_deliveries(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer, p_per_org integer, p_lock_seconds integer, p_max_attempts integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.claim_due_deliveries(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer, p_per_org integer, p_lock_seconds integer, p_max_attempts integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, organization_id uuid, attempts integer, locked_until timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  UPDATE notification_deliveries d
     SET status = CASE WHEN d.attempts >= p_max_attempts THEN 'dead' ELSE 'retrying' END,
         next_attempt_at = CASE WHEN d.attempts >= p_max_attempts THEN d.next_attempt_at
                                ELSE p_now + make_interval(mins => (ARRAY[1,5,30,120,360])[least(greatest(d.attempts, 1), 5)]) END,
         locked_until = NULL, last_error_code = 'PROVIDER_TEMPORARY'
   WHERE d.status = 'sending' AND d.locked_until < p_now;
  RETURN QUERY
  WITH due_orgs AS (
    SELECT d.organization_id FROM notification_deliveries d
     WHERE d.status IN ('queued','retrying') AND d.next_attempt_at <= p_now AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = d.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
     GROUP BY d.organization_id
     ORDER BY min(d.next_attempt_at), d.organization_id
     LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 1000)
  ), locked AS (
    SELECT x.id, x.organization_id, x.next_attempt_at
      FROM due_orgs o
      CROSS JOIN LATERAL (
        SELECT d.id, d.organization_id, d.next_attempt_at FROM notification_deliveries d
         WHERE d.organization_id = o.organization_id AND d.status IN ('queued','retrying') AND d.next_attempt_at <= p_now
         ORDER BY d.next_attempt_at, d.id
         LIMIT greatest(p_per_org, 1)
         FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
      ) x
  ), ranked AS (
    SELECT l.id, l.next_attempt_at,
           row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY l.organization_id ORDER BY l.next_attempt_at, l.id) AS rn
      FROM locked l
  ), picked AS (
    SELECT r.id FROM ranked r ORDER BY r.rn, r.next_attempt_at, r.id LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 1000)
  )
  UPDATE notification_deliveries d
     SET status = 'sending', attempts = d.attempts + 1,
         locked_until = date_trunc('milliseconds', p_now) + make_interval(secs => greatest(p_lock_seconds, 1))
    FROM picked p WHERE d.id = p.id
  RETURNING d.id, d.organization_id, d.attempts, d.locked_until;
END $function$
-- app.current_org() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.current_org()
 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE
AS $function$
DECLARE v text := current_setting('app.org_id', true);
BEGIN
  IF v IS NULL OR v = '' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'app.org_id chưa đặt — thiếu ngữ cảnh tổ chức' USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'TENANT_CONTEXT_MISSING';
  END IF;
  RETURN v::uuid;
END $function$

 RETURNS uuid
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE
AS $function$

BEGIN
  IF v IS NULL OR v = '' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'app.user_id is not set — missing user context' USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'USER_CONTEXT_MISSING';
  END IF;

END $function$
-- app.db_health_metrics() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.db_health_metrics()
 RETURNS TABLE(name text, labels jsonb, value double precision)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_db_connections'::text, '{}'::jsonb, d.numbackends::double precision
    FROM pg_stat_database d WHERE d.datname = current_database();
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_db_max_connections'::text, '{}'::jsonb, current_setting('max_connections')::double precision;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_db_xid_age'::text, '{}'::jsonb, age(d.datfrozenxid)::double precision
    FROM pg_database d WHERE d.datname = current_database();
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_db_multixact_age'::text, '{}'::jsonb, mxid_age(d.datminmxid)::double precision
    FROM pg_database d WHERE d.datname = current_database();
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_db_dead_tuples'::text, jsonb_build_object('table', s.relname::text), s.n_dead_tup::double precision
    FROM pg_stat_user_tables s WHERE s.schemaname = 'public';
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_db_dead_tuple_ratio'::text, jsonb_build_object('table', s.relname::text),
    CASE WHEN s.n_live_tup + s.n_dead_tup = 0 THEN 0 ELSE s.n_dead_tup::double precision / (s.n_live_tup + s.n_dead_tup) END
    FROM pg_stat_user_tables s WHERE s.schemaname = 'public';
END $function$
-- app.dead_letter_jobs() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dead_letter_jobs()
 RETURNS bigint
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE n bigint;
BEGIN
  IF to_regclass('pgboss.job') IS NULL THEN RETURN 0; END IF;
  EXECUTE 'SELECT count(*) FROM pgboss.job WHERE name = ''outbox.dead_letter'' AND state IN (''created'', ''retry'', ''active'', ''failed'')' INTO n;
  RETURN n;
END $function$
-- app.deposit_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.deposit_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['amount','deducted_total','refunded_total','balance','updated_at','version'])
     <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['amount','deducted_total','refunded_total','balance','updated_at','version']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'cọc %: không sửa đối tượng/người cọc', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.dsar_claim(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_stale_minutes integer, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_claim(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_stale_minutes integer, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, subject_user_id uuid, type text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    WITH due AS (
      SELECT r.id FROM dsar_requests r
      WHERE r.type IN ('access', 'delete')
        AND (r.status = 'verified'
             OR (r.status = 'processing'
                 AND r.processing_started_at < least(p_now, now()) - make_interval(mins => greatest(p_stale_minutes, 1))))
      ORDER BY r.due_at, r.id
      LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 100)
      FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    )
    UPDATE dsar_requests r SET status = 'processing', processing_started_at = now(), updated_at = now(),
           pending_file_key = CASE WHEN r.type = 'access'
                                   THEN 'dsar/' || r.subject_user_id::text || '/' || r.id::text || '.json' END
      FROM due WHERE r.id = due.id
    RETURNING r.id, r.subject_user_id, r.type
  $function$
-- app.dsar_clear_result(p_id uuid, p_key text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_clear_result(p_id uuid, p_key text)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    WITH x AS (
      UPDATE dsar_requests r
         SET result_file_key = CASE WHEN r.result_file_key = p_key AND r.result_expires_at <= now()
                                    THEN NULL ELSE r.result_file_key END,
             result_expires_at = CASE WHEN r.result_file_key = p_key AND r.result_expires_at <= now()
                                      THEN NULL ELSE r.result_expires_at END,
             pending_file_key = CASE WHEN r.pending_file_key = p_key AND r.status IN ('completed', 'rejected')
                                     THEN NULL ELSE r.pending_file_key END,
             updated_at = now()
       WHERE r.id = p_id
         AND ((r.result_file_key = p_key AND r.result_expires_at <= now())
              OR (r.pending_file_key = p_key AND r.status IN ('completed', 'rejected')))
      RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*) = 1 FROM x
  $function$
-- app.dsar_complete_access(p_request_id uuid, p_key text, p_expires_at timestamp with time zone, p_summary jsonb) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_complete_access(p_request_id uuid, p_key text, p_expires_at timestamp with time zone, p_summary jsonb)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    WITH x AS (
      UPDATE dsar_requests r
         SET status = 'completed', completed_at = now(), result_file_key = p_key,
             result_expires_at = least(greatest(p_expires_at, now() + interval '1 minute'), now() + interval '7 days'),
             result_summary = p_summary, pending_file_key = NULL, updated_at = now()
       WHERE r.id = p_request_id AND r.type = 'access' AND r.status = 'processing'
      RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*) = 1 FROM x
  $function$
-- app.dsar_due_results(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_due_results(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, result_file_key text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    SELECT x.id, x.k FROM (
      SELECT r.id, r.result_file_key AS k, r.result_expires_at AS at FROM dsar_requests r
       WHERE r.result_file_key IS NOT NULL AND r.result_expires_at <= least(p_now, now())
      UNION ALL
      -- Package written (or maybe written) for a request that closed without completing: delete it too.
      SELECT r.id, r.pending_file_key, r.updated_at FROM dsar_requests r
       WHERE r.pending_file_key IS NOT NULL AND r.status IN ('completed', 'rejected')
    ) x
     ORDER BY x.at, x.id
     LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 1000)
  $function$
-- app.dsar_erase_account(p_request_id uuid, p_note text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_erase_account(p_request_id uuid, p_note text)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_user uuid;
  v_email text;
  v_sole integer;
  n_sessions integer;
  n_accounts integer;
  n_mfa integer;
  n_members integer;
  n_events integer;
  n_files integer;
  v_summary jsonb;
BEGIN
  IF p_note IS NULL OR btrim(p_note) = '' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'dsar_erase_account: legal retention note is required' USING ERRCODE = '22023';
  END IF;
  SELECT r.subject_user_id INTO v_user FROM dsar_requests r
   WHERE r.id = p_request_id AND r.type = 'delete' AND r.status = 'processing'
   FOR UPDATE;
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  SELECT u.email INTO v_email FROM auth."user" u WHERE u.id = v_user FOR UPDATE;
  -- Own memberships + every active owner of our orgs, ONE statement in id order (lockTarget convention, no deadlock): a concurrent
  -- erase of the other sole owner waits here; the count below (new snapshot) then sees its revocation — no write-skew to 0 owners.
  PERFORM 1 FROM memberships m
   WHERE m.auth_user_id = v_user
      OR (m.role = 'owner' AND m.status = 'active'
          AND m.organization_id IN (SELECT x.organization_id FROM memberships x WHERE x.auth_user_id = v_user))
   ORDER BY m.id FOR UPDATE;
  PERFORM 1 FROM dsar_requests r WHERE r.subject_user_id = v_user ORDER BY r.id FOR UPDATE;

  SELECT count(*)::int INTO v_sole FROM memberships m
   WHERE m.auth_user_id = v_user AND m.role = 'owner' AND m.status = 'active'
     AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memberships o
                     WHERE o.organization_id = m.organization_id AND o.role = 'owner' AND o.status = 'active'
                       AND o.id <> m.id);
  IF v_sole > 0 THEN
    UPDATE dsar_requests r
       SET status = 'rejected', completed_at = now(), updated_at = now(),
           result_summary = jsonb_build_object('reason', 'last_owner', 'organizations', v_sole)
     WHERE r.id = p_request_id;
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'rejected', 'reason', 'last_owner', 'organizations', v_sole);
  END IF;

  INSERT INTO auth.revoked_session (token_hash, user_id, expires_at)
  SELECT sha256(convert_to(s.token, 'UTF8')), s."userId", s."expiresAt" FROM auth."session" s WHERE s."userId" = v_user
  ON CONFLICT (token_hash) DO NOTHING;
  DELETE FROM auth."session" s WHERE s."userId" = v_user;
  GET DIAGNOSTICS n_sessions = ROW_COUNT;
  DELETE FROM auth.account a WHERE a."userId" = v_user;
  GET DIAGNOSTICS n_accounts = ROW_COUNT;
  DELETE FROM auth."twoFactor" f WHERE f."userId" = v_user;
  GET DIAGNOSTICS n_mfa = ROW_COUNT;
  DELETE FROM auth.verification v WHERE v.value = v_user::text;
  IF v_email IS NOT NULL THEN
    DELETE FROM auth.verification v
     WHERE lower(v.identifier) LIKE '%-otp-%'
       AND right(lower(v.identifier), char_length(v_email) + 5) = '-otp-' || lower(v_email);
    DELETE FROM auth.login_throttle t WHERE t.email_hash = sha256(convert_to(lower(btrim(v_email)), 'UTF8'));
  END IF;
  UPDATE auth."user" u
     SET email = 'deleted+' || gen_random_uuid()::text || '@deleted.invalid', name = '***', image = NULL,
         "emailVerified" = false, "twoFactorEnabled" = false, "updatedAt" = now()
   WHERE u.id = v_user;

  -- Deferred last-owner check must run here (definer rights); at COMMIT it would run as app_worker and hit memberships RLS.
  SET CONSTRAINTS trg_memberships_last_owner IMMEDIATE;
  WITH rev AS (
    UPDATE memberships m SET status = 'revoked', revoked_at = now(), version = m.version + 1
     WHERE m.auth_user_id = v_user AND m.status IN ('invited', 'active', 'suspended')
    RETURNING m.id, m.organization_id
  ), aud AS (
    INSERT INTO audit_events (id, organization_id, actor_type, action, entity_type, entity_id, reason, correlation_id)
    SELECT gen_random_uuid(), rev.organization_id, 'worker', 'member.account_deleted', 'membership', rev.id,
           'dsar_delete', 'dsar:' || p_request_id::text
      FROM rev
    RETURNING 1
  )
  SELECT count(*)::int INTO n_members FROM rev;

  n_events := app.unlink_product_events_actor(v_user);

  WITH mine AS (
    SELECT j.organization_id, j.id FROM export_jobs j
      JOIN memberships m ON m.organization_id = j.organization_id AND m.id = j.requested_by
     WHERE m.auth_user_id = v_user
  ), upd AS (
    UPDATE files f
       SET delete_after = (now() AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date
      FROM mine
     WHERE f.organization_id = mine.organization_id AND f.owner_type = 'export_job' AND f.owner_id = mine.id
       AND f.status IN ('ready', 'pending_scan')
       AND (f.delete_after IS NULL OR f.delete_after > (now() AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date)
    RETURNING 1
  )
  SELECT count(*)::int INTO n_files FROM upd;

  -- 1 minute back: the cleanup right after (worker clock may lag the DB clock) sees the package as expired.
  UPDATE dsar_requests r SET result_expires_at = now() - interval '1 minute', updated_at = now()
   WHERE r.subject_user_id = v_user AND r.result_file_key IS NOT NULL AND r.result_expires_at > now() - interval '1 minute';
  UPDATE dsar_requests r
     SET status = 'rejected', completed_at = now(), updated_at = now(),
         result_summary = jsonb_build_object('reason', 'account_deleted')
   WHERE r.subject_user_id = v_user AND r.id <> p_request_id AND r.status IN ('received', 'verified', 'processing');

  v_summary := jsonb_build_object(
    'sessions_revoked', n_sessions, 'sign_in_methods_removed', n_accounts, 'mfa_removed', n_mfa,
    'memberships_revoked', n_members, 'product_events_unlinked', n_events, 'files_marked_for_deletion', n_files,
    'kept', jsonb_build_array('invoices', 'ledger', 'audit_events', 'consent_records', 'persons'));
  UPDATE dsar_requests r
     SET status = 'completed', completed_at = now(), updated_at = now(), result_summary = v_summary,
         legal_retention_note = p_note
   WHERE r.id = p_request_id;
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('status', 'completed') || v_summary;
END $function$
-- app.dsar_export_data(p_request_id uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_export_data(p_request_id uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_user uuid;
BEGIN
  SELECT r.subject_user_id INTO v_user FROM dsar_requests r
   WHERE r.id = p_request_id AND r.type = 'access' AND r.status = 'processing';
  IF v_user IS NULL THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  RETURN jsonb_build_object(
    'format', 'hm-dsar-access/1',
    'generated_at', now(),
    'request_id', p_request_id,
    'account', (
      SELECT jsonb_build_object('id', u.id, 'email', u.email, 'name', u.name, 'email_verified', u."emailVerified",
                                'two_factor_enabled', coalesce(u."twoFactorEnabled", false), 'image', u.image,
                                'created_at', u."createdAt", 'updated_at', u."updatedAt")
        FROM auth."user" u WHERE u.id = v_user),
    'sign_in_methods', coalesce((
      SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('provider', a."providerId", 'created_at', a."createdAt",
                                          'updated_at', a."updatedAt") ORDER BY a."createdAt", a.id)
        FROM auth.account a WHERE a."userId" = v_user), '[]'::jsonb),
    'sessions', coalesce((
      SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('created_at', s."createdAt", 'expires_at', s."expiresAt",
                                          'ip_address', s."ipAddress", 'user_agent', s."userAgent")
                       ORDER BY s."createdAt", s.id)
        FROM auth."session" s WHERE s."userId" = v_user), '[]'::jsonb),
    'memberships', coalesce((
      SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object(
               'id', m.id, 'organization_id', m.organization_id, 'organization_name', o.name, 'role', m.role,
               'status', m.status, 'valid_from', m.valid_from, 'valid_to', m.valid_to, 'revoked_at', m.revoked_at,
               'created_at', m.created_at,
               'person', jsonb_build_object('id', p.id, 'full_name', p.full_name, 'email', p.email, 'phone', p.phone,
                                            'address', p.address, 'nationality', p.nationality,
                                            'anonymized_at', p.anonymized_at))
             ORDER BY m.created_at, m.id)
        FROM memberships m
        JOIN organizations o ON o.id = m.organization_id
        JOIN persons p ON p.organization_id = m.organization_id AND p.id = m.person_id
       WHERE m.auth_user_id = v_user), '[]'::jsonb),
    'consent_records', coalesce((
      SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', c.id, 'purpose', c.purpose, 'action', c.action,
                                          'policy_version', c.policy_version, 'channel', c.channel,
                                          'ui_snapshot_hash', c.ui_snapshot_hash, 'recorded_at', c.recorded_at)
                       ORDER BY c.seq)
        FROM consent_records c WHERE c.subject_user_id = v_user), '[]'::jsonb),
    'data_requests', coalesce((
      SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('id', d.id, 'type', d.type, 'status', d.status, 'region', d.region,
                                          'requested_at', d.requested_at, 'due_at', d.due_at,
                                          'completed_at', d.completed_at)
                       ORDER BY d.requested_at, d.id)
        FROM dsar_requests d WHERE d.subject_user_id = v_user), '[]'::jsonb),
    'processing_restriction', (
      SELECT jsonb_build_object('restricted_at', x.restricted_at)
        FROM processing_restrictions x WHERE x.subject_user_id = v_user),
    'product_events', coalesce((
      SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('organization_id', e.organization_id, 'property_id', e.property_id,
                                          'name', e.name, 'schema_version', e.schema_version, 'props', e.props,
                                          'occurred_at', e.occurred_at) ORDER BY e.occurred_at, e.id)
        FROM product_events e WHERE e.actor_user_id = v_user), '[]'::jsonb)
  );
END $function$
-- app.dsar_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(name text, labels jsonb, value double precision)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    SELECT 'hm_dsar_overdue'::text, '{}'::jsonb, count(*)::double precision
      FROM dsar_requests r
     WHERE r.status IN ('received', 'verified', 'processing') AND r.due_at < p_now
  $function$
-- app.dsar_sole_owner_orgs() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.dsar_sole_owner_orgs()
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM memberships m
    WHERE m.auth_user_id = app.current_user_id() AND m.role = 'owner' AND m.status = 'active'
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memberships o
                      WHERE o.organization_id = m.organization_id AND o.role = 'owner' AND o.status = 'active'
                        AND o.id <> m.id)
  $function$
-- app.due_held_reservations(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.due_held_reservations(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(reservation_id uuid, organization_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT r.id, r.organization_id FROM reservations r
    JOIN reservation_items i ON i.organization_id = r.organization_id AND i.reservation_id = r.id
    LEFT JOIN allocations a ON a.organization_id = i.organization_id AND a.id = i.allocation_id
    WHERE r.status = 'held' AND (a.id IS NULL OR NOT a.active OR a.expires_at <= p_now) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = r.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
    ORDER BY r.held_until, r.id
    LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 10000)
  $function$
-- app.email_budget_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_budget integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.email_budget_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_budget integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(name text, labels jsonb, value double precision)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT 'hm_email_budget_used_ratio'::text, '{}'::jsonb,
           coalesce((SELECT max(b.used) FROM email_budget_days b WHERE b.day = (p_now AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date), 0)::double precision
             / greatest(p_budget, 1)
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'hm_email_budget_throttled_today'::text, '{}'::jsonb, coalesce(sum(b.throttled), 0)::double precision
      FROM email_budget_days b WHERE b.day = (p_now AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date
    UNION ALL
    SELECT 'hm_email_budget_stopped_today'::text, '{}'::jsonb, coalesce(sum(b.stopped), 0)::double precision
      FROM email_budget_days b WHERE b.day = (p_now AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date
  $function$
-- app.email_budget_take(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone, p_budget integer, p_warn_pct integer, p_throttle_pct integer, p_required boolean) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.email_budget_take(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone, p_budget integer, p_warn_pct integer, p_throttle_pct integer, p_required boolean)
 RETURNS TABLE(decision text, used integer, level integer, crossed boolean)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  d date := (p_now AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')::date;
  r email_budget_days%ROWTYPE;
  n integer;
  lv integer;
BEGIN
  IF p_budget IS NULL OR p_budget < 1 OR p_warn_pct IS NULL OR p_throttle_pct IS NULL
     OR p_warn_pct < 1 OR p_throttle_pct < p_warn_pct OR p_throttle_pct > 100 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'email_budget_take: tham số ngân sách không hợp lệ' USING ERRCODE = '22023';
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO email_budget_days (organization_id, day) VALUES (p_org, d) ON CONFLICT (organization_id, day) DO NOTHING;
  SELECT * INTO r FROM email_budget_days b WHERE b.organization_id = p_org AND b.day = d FOR UPDATE;
  IF r.used >= p_budget THEN
    UPDATE email_budget_days b SET stopped = b.stopped + 1, updated_at = p_now WHERE b.organization_id = p_org AND b.day = d;
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'stopped'::text, r.used, r.level::integer, false;
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  IF NOT p_required AND r.used::bigint * 100 >= p_throttle_pct::bigint * p_budget THEN
    UPDATE email_budget_days b SET throttled = b.throttled + 1, updated_at = p_now WHERE b.organization_id = p_org AND b.day = d;
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'throttled'::text, r.used, r.level::integer, false;
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  n := r.used + 1;
  lv := CASE WHEN n >= p_budget THEN 3
             WHEN n::bigint * 100 >= p_throttle_pct::bigint * p_budget THEN 2
             WHEN n::bigint * 100 >= p_warn_pct::bigint * p_budget THEN 1
             ELSE 0 END;
  UPDATE email_budget_days b SET used = n, level = greatest(b.level, lv), updated_at = p_now
   WHERE b.organization_id = p_org AND b.day = d;
  DELETE FROM email_budget_days x WHERE x.ctid IN (
    SELECT y.ctid FROM email_budget_days y WHERE y.day < d - 90 LIMIT 50 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED);
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'send'::text, n, greatest(r.level::integer, lv), lv > r.level;
END $function$
-- app.enforce_plan_quota() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.enforce_plan_quota()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_kind text := TG_ARGV[0];
  v_plan text;
  v_limit bigint;
  v_used bigint;
  v_add bigint := 1;
BEGIN
  -- Does the new/changed row INCREASE usage? If not, skip (no lock).
  IF v_kind IN ('properties', 'units') THEN
    IF NEW.archived_at IS NOT NULL OR (TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.archived_at IS NULL) THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
  ELSIF v_kind = 'members' AND TG_TABLE_NAME = 'memberships' THEN
    IF NOT (NEW.status = 'active' AND NEW.role NOT IN ('tenant', 'platform_support'))
       OR (TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.status = 'active' AND OLD.role NOT IN ('tenant', 'platform_support')) THEN
      RETURN NEW;
    END IF;
  ELSIF v_kind = 'members' THEN
    IF NEW.status <> 'pending' OR NEW.role IN ('tenant', 'platform_support') THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
  ELSIF v_kind = 'storage' THEN
    IF NEW.status NOT IN ('pending_scan', 'ready') OR NEW.owner_type = 'export_job' THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
    -- Staging replay (INSERT ... ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING, same id): row already counted.
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM files f WHERE f.id = NEW.id) THEN RETURN NEW; END IF;
    v_add := NEW.size_bytes;
  ELSE
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'app.enforce_plan_quota: unknown quota kind %', v_kind;
  END IF;

  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('plan_quota:' || v_kind || ':' || NEW.organization_id::text, 0));
  SELECT o.plan INTO v_plan FROM organizations o WHERE o.id = NEW.organization_id;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN RETURN NEW; END IF; -- the FK reports the proper error afterwards.
  SELECT CASE v_kind WHEN 'properties' THEN l.max_properties WHEN 'units' THEN l.max_units
                     WHEN 'members' THEN l.max_active_members ELSE l.max_storage_bytes END
    INTO v_limit FROM app.plan_limits(v_plan) l;
  IF v_limit IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'plan % missing from app.plan_limits', v_plan;
  END IF;

  -- NEW is not counted yet: BEFORE INSERT row is not in the table; UPDATE only gets here when OLD was not counted.
  -- Counts use indexes (uq_units_code / ix_properties_org_archived cover non-archived rows).
  IF v_kind = 'properties' THEN
    SELECT count(*) INTO v_used FROM properties p
     WHERE p.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND p.archived_at IS NULL;
  ELSIF v_kind = 'units' THEN
    SELECT count(*) INTO v_used FROM units u
     WHERE u.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND u.archived_at IS NULL;
  ELSIF v_kind = 'members' THEN
    SELECT count(*) INTO v_used FROM memberships m
     WHERE m.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND m.status = 'active'
       AND m.role NOT IN ('tenant', 'platform_support');
    IF TG_TABLE_NAME = 'invitations' THEN
      v_used := v_used + (SELECT count(*) FROM invitations i
                           WHERE i.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND i.status = 'pending' AND i.expires_at > now()
                             AND i.role NOT IN ('tenant', 'platform_support'));
    END IF;
  ELSE
    SELECT coalesce(sum(f.size_bytes), 0) INTO v_used FROM files f
     WHERE f.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND f.status IN ('pending_scan', 'ready') AND f.owner_type <> 'export_job';
  END IF;

  IF v_used + v_add > v_limit THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'plan quota exceeded: % (plan %, limit %, used %)', v_kind, v_plan, v_limit, v_used
      USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'QUOTA_EXCEEDED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.expire_export_jobs(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.expire_export_jobs(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH x AS (UPDATE export_jobs SET status = 'expired' WHERE status = 'ready' AND expires_at <= p_now RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM x
  $function$
-- app.expire_holds(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.expire_holds(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(allocation_id uuid, organization_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH due AS (
      SELECT a.id FROM allocations a
      WHERE a.active AND a.kind = 'hold' AND a.expires_at <= p_now AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = a.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
      ORDER BY a.expires_at, a.id
      LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 10000)
      FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    )
    UPDATE allocations a SET active = false, deactivated_at = p_now, deactivated_reason = 'expired'
    FROM due WHERE a.id = due.id AND a.active
    RETURNING a.id, a.organization_id
  $function$
-- app.export_job_transition() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.export_job_transition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.organization_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.organization_id OR NEW.requested_by IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.requested_by
     OR NEW.kind IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.kind OR NEW.format IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.format
     OR NEW.filters IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.filters OR NEW.scope_snapshot IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.scope_snapshot
     OR NEW.property_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.property_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'export_jobs %: không đổi nội dung yêu cầu', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status AND NOT (
       (OLD.status = 'queued' AND NEW.status IN ('running','failed'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'running' AND NEW.status IN ('ready','failed'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'ready' AND NEW.status = 'expired')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'export_jobs %: không chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status
      USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.files_due_for_deletion(p_today date, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.files_due_for_deletion(p_today date, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, organization_id uuid, storage_key text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT f.id, f.organization_id, f.storage_key FROM files f
    WHERE f.status IN ('ready', 'pending_scan') AND f.delete_after IS NOT NULL AND f.delete_after <= p_today
    ORDER BY f.delete_after, f.id
    LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 1000)
  $function$
-- app.forbid_mutation() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.forbid_mutation()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(CASE WHEN TG_LEVEL = 'ROW' THEN (to_jsonb(OLD) ->> 'organization_id')::uuid ELSE nullif(current_setting('app.purge_org', true), '')::uuid END) THEN IF TG_LEVEL = 'ROW' THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; RETURN NULL; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  RAISE EXCEPTION 'bảng % là append-only (%)', TG_TABLE_NAME, TG_OP USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
END $function$
-- app.forbid_reactivation() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.forbid_reactivation()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.active = false AND NEW.active = true THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'allocation % đã nhả, không kích hoạt lại', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.guest_link_attempt(p_id uuid, p_ok boolean, p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.guest_link_attempt(p_id uuid, p_ok boolean, p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE locked boolean;
BEGIN
  IF p_ok THEN
    -- Kiểm lại khoá dưới khoá hàng: lần đúng chạy song song sau lần sai thứ 5 vẫn bị từ chối (review lát 4).
    UPDATE guest_access_links SET last_used_at = p_now WHERE id = p_id AND locked_at IS NULL AND revoked_at IS NULL;
    RETURN NOT FOUND;
  END IF;
  UPDATE guest_access_links SET failed_attempts = failed_attempts + 1,
         locked_at = CASE WHEN failed_attempts + 1 >= 5 THEN coalesce(locked_at, p_now) ELSE locked_at END
   WHERE id = p_id RETURNING locked_at IS NOT NULL INTO locked;
  RETURN coalesce(locked, false);
END $function$
-- app.guest_link_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.guest_link_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['revoked_at','revoked_by','last_used_at','failed_attempts','locked_at','updated_at','version'])
     IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['revoked_at','revoked_by','last_used_at','failed_attempts','locked_at','updated_at','version']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'link khách %: chỉ đổi thu hồi/khoá', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.revoked_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.revoked_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.revoked_at THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'link khách % đã thu hồi', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.guest_link_rate(p_ip_hash bytea, p_now timestamp with time zone, p_record boolean) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.guest_link_rate(p_ip_hash bytea, p_now timestamp with time zone, p_record boolean)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE w timestamptz := date_trunc('minute', p_now); n integer;
BEGIN
  IF p_record THEN
    INSERT INTO guest_link_rate (ip_hash, window_start, failures) VALUES (p_ip_hash, w, 1)
    ON CONFLICT (ip_hash, window_start) DO UPDATE SET failures = guest_link_rate.failures + 1
    RETURNING failures INTO n;
    DELETE FROM guest_link_rate WHERE window_start < w - interval '10 minutes';
    RETURN n;
  END IF;
  SELECT failures INTO n FROM guest_link_rate WHERE ip_hash = p_ip_hash AND window_start = w;
  RETURN coalesce(n, 0);
END $function$
-- app.guest_stay_view(p_link uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.guest_stay_view(p_link uuid)
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH g AS (SELECT * FROM app.resolve_guest_link((SELECT token_hash FROM guest_access_links WHERE id = p_link))),
    st AS (
      SELECT s.id, s.status, s.organization_id, ri.arrival_date, ri.departure_date, u.code AS unit_code, r.number, r.id AS reservation_id,
             r.booker_person_id, s.property_id
      FROM g JOIN stays s ON s.organization_id = g.organization_id AND s.id = g.stay_id
      JOIN reservation_items ri ON ri.organization_id = s.organization_id AND ri.id = s.reservation_item_id
      JOIN reservations r ON r.organization_id = ri.organization_id AND r.id = ri.reservation_id
      JOIN units u ON u.organization_id = s.organization_id AND u.id = s.unit_id
    )
    SELECT jsonb_build_object(
      'stay', jsonb_build_object('id', st.id, 'status', st.status, 'arrivalDate', st.arrival_date, 'departureDate', st.departure_date,
                                 'unitCode', st.unit_code, 'reservationNumber', st.number),
      'charges', coalesce((SELECT jsonb_agg(jsonb_build_object('description', c.description, 'businessDate', c.business_date,
                                                               'amount', c.amount) ORDER BY c.business_date, c.created_at)
                           FROM charges c WHERE c.organization_id = st.organization_id AND c.stay_id = st.id AND c.voided_at IS NULL), '[]'),
      'invoice', (SELECT jsonb_build_object('total', i.total, 'allocated', i.allocated_total) FROM invoices i
                  WHERE i.organization_id = st.organization_id AND i.stay_id = st.id AND i.kind = 'folio' LIMIT 1),
      -- Ứng trước chưa phân bổ, chặn trần theo số dư CUSTOMER_CREDIT của TỪNG người trả (cộng dồn rồi mới chặn — review lát 4).
      'advancePaid', coalesce((SELECT sum(least(u.unallocated, greatest(0, (
                                 SELECT coalesce(sum(jl.credit - jl.debit), 0) FROM journal_lines jl
                                 WHERE jl.organization_id = st.organization_id AND jl.account = 'CUSTOMER_CREDIT'
                                   AND jl.person_id = u.payer_person_id AND jl.property_id = st.property_id))))
                               FROM (SELECT p.payer_person_id, sum(p.amount - p.allocated_total) AS unallocated
                                     FROM payments p WHERE p.organization_id = st.organization_id
                                       AND p.reservation_id = st.reservation_id
                                       AND p.purpose = 'receivable' AND p.status = 'confirmed'
                                     GROUP BY p.payer_person_id) u), 0))
    FROM st
  $function$
-- app.handover_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.handover_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'biên bản bàn giao không xoá được' USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.confirmed_at IS NOT NULL AND (
       (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['readings_pending','confirmed_by_tenant_at','confirmation_method','updated_at','version'])
         <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['readings_pending','confirmed_by_tenant_at','confirmation_method','updated_at','version'])
       OR (NEW.readings_pending AND NOT OLD.readings_pending)
       OR (OLD.confirmed_by_tenant_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.confirmed_by_tenant_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.confirmed_by_tenant_at)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'biên bản bàn giao % đã xác nhận — không sửa', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.handover_reading_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.handover_reading_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  IF OLD.handover_id IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM handovers h WHERE h.organization_id = OLD.organization_id
       AND h.id = OLD.handover_id AND h.confirmed_at IS NOT NULL) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'chỉ số của biên bản bàn giao đã xác nhận — không sửa/xoá' USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN RETURN OLD; END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.import_batch_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.import_batch_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE allowed text[];
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lô nhập % không xoá được', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.status IN ('committed','cancelled') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lô nhập % đã %', OLD.id, OLD.status USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.kind <> OLD.kind OR NEW.property_id <> OLD.property_id OR NEW.file_id <> OLD.file_id
     OR NEW.file_sha256 <> OLD.file_sha256 OR NEW.uploaded_by <> OLD.uploaded_by OR NEW.cutoff_on IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.cutoff_on
     OR NEW.header <> OLD.header THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lô nhập %: không đổi nguồn', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status <> OLD.status THEN
    allowed := CASE OLD.status
      WHEN 'uploaded' THEN ARRAY['validated','cancelled']
      WHEN 'validated' THEN ARRAY['validated','confirmed','cancelled']
      WHEN 'confirmed' THEN ARRAY['validated','committed','cancelled']
      ELSE ARRAY[]::text[] END;
    IF NOT NEW.status = ANY (allowed) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'lô nhập %: không thể chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status
        USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
    END IF;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.import_row_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.import_row_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE st text;
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  SELECT status INTO st FROM import_batches WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND id = OLD.batch_id;
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'dòng nhập không xoá được' USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF st IN ('committed','cancelled') AND ((to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['raw','data','updated_at','version'])
       <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['raw','data','updated_at','version']) OR NEW.raw <> '{}'::jsonb OR NEW.data IS NOT NULL) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lô nhập đã % — dòng bất biến', st USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.inbox_immutable() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.inbox_immutable()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.provider IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.provider OR NEW.account IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.account
     OR NEW.provider_event_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.provider_event_id OR NEW.payload_hash IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.payload_hash
     OR NEW.raw IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.raw OR NEW.organization_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.organization_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'nội dung inbox % không được sửa', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.bank_transaction_id IS NOT NULL AND NEW.bank_transaction_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.bank_transaction_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'inbox % đã gắn giao dịch ngân hàng', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
