-- app.invoice_immutable() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.invoice_immutable()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['allocated_total','adjusted_total','open_amount','updated_at','version'])
     <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['allocated_total','adjusted_total','open_amount','updated_at','version']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiếu % đã phát hành — không sửa được; dùng phiếu điều chỉnh (INV-G4)', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  -- Điều chỉnh chỉ cộng dồn giảm (≤ 0), không hoàn tác bằng UPDATE.
  IF NEW.adjusted_total > OLD.adjusted_total THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiếu %: điều chỉnh chỉ cộng dồn', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.lease_activation_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_activation_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  -- Chỉ áp cho đúng bước signed_awaiting_handover → active; chuyển sai bảng để trg_leases_guard báo INVALID_TRANSITION.
  IF NEW.status = 'active' AND OLD.status = 'signed_awaiting_handover' AND NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM handovers h WHERE h.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND h.lease_id = NEW.id
         AND h.kind = 'move_in' AND h.confirmed_at IS NOT NULL) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hợp đồng %: cần biên bản bàn giao nhận phòng trước khi kích hoạt', NEW.id
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'HANDOVER_REQUIRED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.lease_close_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_close_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.status = 'closed' AND OLD.status = 'settling' AND NOT EXISTS (
       SELECT 1 FROM lease_settlements s WHERE s.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND s.lease_id = NEW.id
         AND s.status = 'approved') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hợp đồng %: cần duyệt quyết toán trước khi đóng', NEW.id
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'SETTLEMENT_REQUIRED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.lease_expiry_candidates(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_expiry_candidates(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(property_id uuid, property_name text, lease_id uuid, version_no integer, unit_code text, effective_to date, days_left integer, representative_person_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT l.property_id, p.name, l.id, v.version_no, u.code, v.effective_to,
           (v.effective_to - (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date)::int, l.representative_person_id
    FROM leases l
    JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = l.organization_id AND p.id = l.property_id
    JOIN units u ON u.organization_id = l.organization_id AND u.id = l.unit_id
    LEFT JOIN notification_settings ns ON ns.organization_id = l.organization_id AND ns.property_id = l.property_id
    JOIN LATERAL (SELECT lv.version_no, lv.effective_to FROM lease_versions lv
                  WHERE lv.organization_id = l.organization_id AND lv.lease_id = l.id AND lv.signed_at IS NOT NULL
                  ORDER BY lv.version_no DESC LIMIT 1) v ON true
    WHERE l.organization_id = p_org AND l.status IN ('active','notice_given') AND v.effective_to IS NOT NULL
      AND (v.effective_to - (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date) = ANY (coalesce(ns.lease_expiry_offsets_days, '{30,7}'))
  $function$
-- app.lease_front_desk_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_front_desk_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF app.actor_role() = 'front_desk' AND (
       NEW.status <> 'settling' OR OLD.status NOT IN ('active','notice_given')
       OR (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['status','moved_out_on','updated_at','version'])
          <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['status','moved_out_on','updated_at','version'])) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lễ tân chỉ ghi nhận trả phòng (chuyển sang thanh lý)' USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'FORBIDDEN';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.lease_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE
  allowed text[];
BEGIN
  IF NEW.unit_id <> OLD.unit_id OR NEW.number <> OLD.number OR NEW.property_id <> OLD.property_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hợp đồng %: không đổi phòng/số', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.status NOT IN ('draft','pending_signature') AND (NEW.representative_person_id <> OLD.representative_person_id
       OR NEW.billing_start_on <> OLD.billing_start_on OR NEW.signed_on IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.signed_on) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'hợp đồng % đã ký: không sửa người đại diện/mốc ngày — lập phụ lục', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status <> OLD.status THEN
    allowed := CASE OLD.status
      WHEN 'draft' THEN ARRAY['pending_signature','cancelled']
      WHEN 'pending_signature' THEN ARRAY['signed_awaiting_handover','cancelled']
      WHEN 'signed_awaiting_handover' THEN ARRAY['active','cancelled']
      WHEN 'active' THEN ARRAY['notice_given','settling']
      WHEN 'notice_given' THEN ARRAY['settling']
      WHEN 'settling' THEN ARRAY['closed']
      ELSE ARRAY[]::text[] END;
    IF NOT NEW.status = ANY (allowed) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'hợp đồng %: không thể chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status
        USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
    END IF;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.lease_occupant_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_occupant_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.to_on IS NOT NULL
     OR (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['to_on','updated_at','version']) <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['to_on','updated_at','version']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'người ở %: chỉ được đặt ngày rời đi một lần', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.lease_settlement_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_settlement_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' OR OLD.status = 'approved' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'quyết toán % đã duyệt — không sửa/xoá', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.lease_version_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.lease_version_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  IF OLD.signed_at IS NOT NULL THEN
    -- D-007: ngoại lệ duy nhất — đặt superseded_on từ NULL đúng một lần, không đổi gì khác.
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND OLD.superseded_on IS NULL AND NEW.superseded_on IS NOT NULL
       AND (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['superseded_on','effective_until','updated_at','version'])
         = (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['superseded_on','effective_until','updated_at','version']) THEN
      RETURN NEW;
    END IF;
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'điều khoản hợp đồng (phiên bản %) đã ký — lập phụ lục', OLD.version_no
      USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN RETURN OLD; END IF;
  IF NEW.lease_id <> OLD.lease_id OR NEW.version_no <> OLD.version_no OR NEW.kind <> OLD.kind THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiên bản %: không đổi khoá', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.superseded_on IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.superseded_on THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiên bản %: superseded_on chỉ đặt cho phiên bản đã ký', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.leases_expiring(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer[]) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.leases_expiring(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer[])
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_id uuid, property_id uuid, lease_id uuid, version_no integer, effective_to date, days_left integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT l.organization_id, l.property_id, l.id, v.version_no, v.effective_to,
           (v.effective_to - (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date)::int
    FROM leases l
    JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = l.organization_id AND p.id = l.property_id
    JOIN LATERAL (SELECT lv.version_no, lv.effective_to FROM lease_versions lv
                  WHERE lv.organization_id = l.organization_id AND lv.lease_id = l.id AND lv.signed_at IS NOT NULL
                  ORDER BY lv.version_no DESC LIMIT 1) v ON true
    WHERE l.status IN ('active','notice_given') AND v.effective_to IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = l.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
      AND (v.effective_to - (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date) = ANY (
        CASE WHEN jsonb_typeof(p.settings -> 'lease_reminder_days') = 'array'
             THEN ARRAY(SELECT x::int FROM jsonb_array_elements_text(p.settings -> 'lease_reminder_days') x
                        WHERE x ~ '^[0-9]{1,4}$')
             ELSE p_days END)
  $function$
-- app.leases_overstaying(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.leases_overstaying(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_id uuid, property_id uuid, lease_id uuid, allocation_id uuid, related_allocation_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT l.organization_id, l.property_id, l.id, a.id,
           (SELECT o.id FROM allocations o WHERE o.organization_id = a.organization_id AND o.unit_id = a.unit_id AND o.active
              AND o.id <> a.id AND lower(o.during) >= upper(a.during) ORDER BY lower(o.during), o.id LIMIT 1)
    FROM leases l
    JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = l.organization_id AND p.id = l.property_id
    JOIN LATERAL (SELECT lv.effective_to FROM lease_versions lv
                  WHERE lv.organization_id = l.organization_id AND lv.lease_id = l.id AND lv.signed_at IS NOT NULL
                  ORDER BY lv.version_no DESC LIMIT 1) v ON true
    JOIN allocations a ON a.organization_id = l.organization_id AND a.source_type = 'lease' AND a.source_id = l.id
                      AND a.active AND a.kind = 'lease'
    WHERE l.status IN ('active','notice_given') AND v.effective_to IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = l.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
      AND v.effective_to < (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM handovers h WHERE h.organization_id = l.organization_id AND h.lease_id = l.id
                        AND h.kind = 'move_out' AND h.confirmed_at IS NOT NULL)
  $function$
-- app.ledger_drift() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.ledger_drift()
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_id uuid, debit_total bigint, credit_total bigint)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT l.organization_id, sum(l.debit)::bigint, sum(l.credit)::bigint
    FROM journal_lines l
    GROUP BY l.organization_id
    HAVING sum(l.debit) <> sum(l.credit)
  $function$
-- app.list_my_organizations(p_auth_user_id uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.list_my_organizations(p_auth_user_id uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_id uuid, organization_name text, membership_id uuid, role text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT o.id, o.name, m.id, m.role
    FROM memberships m JOIN organizations o ON o.id = m.organization_id
    WHERE m.auth_user_id = p_auth_user_id AND m.status = 'active' AND o.status IN ('active', 'pending_deletion')
      AND m.valid_from <= now() AND (m.valid_to IS NULL OR m.valid_to > now())
    ORDER BY o.name
  $function$
-- app.locked_through(p_org uuid, p_property uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.locked_through(p_org uuid, p_property uuid)
 RETURNS date
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
AS $function$
  SELECT max(locked_through) FROM period_locks
  WHERE organization_id = p_org AND property_id = p_property AND released_at IS NULL
$function$
-- app.mark_file_deleted(p_id uuid, p_today date) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.mark_file_deleted(p_id uuid, p_today date)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH x AS (
      UPDATE files SET status = 'deleted', deleted_at = now(), version = version + 1
      WHERE id = p_id AND status IN ('ready', 'pending_scan') AND delete_after IS NOT NULL AND delete_after <= p_today
      RETURNING 1)
    SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM x)
  $function$
-- app.memberships_elsewhere(p_auth_user_id uuid, p_org uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.memberships_elsewhere(p_auth_user_id uuid, p_org uuid)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM memberships m
    WHERE m.auth_user_id = p_auth_user_id AND m.organization_id <> p_org AND m.status <> 'revoked'
  $function$
-- app.meter_reading_accountant_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.meter_reading_accountant_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF app.actor_role() = 'accountant' AND ((to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['status','approved_by','version','updated_at'])
       <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['status','approved_by','version','updated_at']) OR NEW.status <> 'approved') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'kế toán chỉ duyệt chỉ số, không sửa' USING ERRCODE = '42501';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.meter_reading_period_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.meter_reading_period_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE st text; pid uuid;
BEGIN
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' AND app.org_purge_active(OLD.organization_id) THEN RETURN OLD; END IF; -- org purge (0070)
  pid := CASE WHEN TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN OLD.billing_period_id ELSE NEW.billing_period_id END;
  IF TG_OP <> 'INSERT' AND OLD.billing_period_id IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT status INTO st FROM billing_periods WHERE organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND id = OLD.billing_period_id;
    IF st IN ('issued','locked') THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'chỉ số thuộc kỳ đã phát hành' USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'PERIOD_ISSUED';
    END IF;
  END IF;
  IF TG_OP <> 'DELETE' AND pid IS NOT NULL THEN
    SELECT status INTO st FROM billing_periods WHERE organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND id = pid;
    IF st IN ('issued','locked') THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'kỳ đã phát hành — không ghi chỉ số' USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'PERIOD_ISSUED';
    END IF;
  END IF;
  IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN RETURN OLD; END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.metrics_snapshot(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.metrics_snapshot(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(name text, labels jsonb, value double precision)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE failed bigint;
BEGIN
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_outbox_unpublished'::text, '{}'::jsonb, count(*)::double precision
    FROM outbox WHERE published_at IS NULL;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_outbox_oldest_unpublished_age_seconds'::text, '{}'::jsonb,
    coalesce(extract(epoch FROM p_now - min(created_at)), 0)::double precision FROM outbox WHERE published_at IS NULL;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_outbox_failing'::text, '{}'::jsonb, count(*)::double precision
    FROM outbox WHERE published_at IS NULL AND attempts > 0;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'notification_dead_total'::text, '{}'::jsonb, count(*)::double precision
    FROM notification_deliveries WHERE status = 'dead';
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_notification_deliveries'::text, jsonb_build_object('status', s.status), coalesce(c.n, 0)::double precision
    FROM unnest(ARRAY['queued','sending','sent','retrying','failed','dead','suppressed']) s(status)
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT d.status, count(*) AS n FROM notification_deliveries d GROUP BY d.status) c ON c.status = s.status;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_export_jobs'::text, jsonb_build_object('status', s.status), coalesce(c.n, 0)::double precision
    FROM unnest(ARRAY['queued','running','ready','failed','expired']) s(status)
    LEFT JOIN (SELECT e.status, count(*) AS n FROM export_jobs e GROUP BY e.status) c ON c.status = s.status;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_holds_expired_total'::text, '{}'::jsonb, count(*)::double precision
    FROM allocations WHERE kind = 'hold' AND deactivated_reason = 'expired';
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_holds_active'::text, '{}'::jsonb, count(*)::double precision
    FROM allocations WHERE kind = 'hold' AND active;
  IF to_regclass('pgboss.job') IS NOT NULL THEN
    EXECUTE 'SELECT count(*) FROM pgboss.job WHERE state = ''failed''' INTO failed;
    RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_jobs_failed'::text, '{}'::jsonb, failed::double precision;
  END IF;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_worker_heartbeat_age_seconds'::text, jsonb_build_object('worker', w.name),
    extract(epoch FROM p_now - w.beat_at)::double precision FROM worker_heartbeats w;
END $function$
-- app.next_number(p_org uuid, p_property uuid, p_key text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.next_number(p_org uuid, p_property uuid, p_key text)
 RETURNS bigint
 LANGUAGE sql
AS $function$
    UPDATE properties
       SET counters = jsonb_set(counters, ARRAY[p_key], to_jsonb(coalesce((counters ->> p_key)::bigint, 0) + 1))
     WHERE organization_id = p_org AND id = p_property
    RETURNING (counters ->> p_key)::bigint
  $function$
-- app.notification_delivery_transition() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.notification_delivery_transition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.notification_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.notification_id OR NEW.channel IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.channel
     OR NEW.organization_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.organization_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lần gửi %: không đổi thông báo/kênh', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status AND NOT (
       (OLD.status IN ('queued','retrying') AND NEW.status IN ('sending','suppressed'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'sending' AND NEW.status IN ('sent','retrying','failed','dead','suppressed'))
    OR (OLD.status IN ('failed','dead') AND NEW.status = 'queued')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lần gửi %: không chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.notification_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.notification_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - 'read_at') IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(OLD) - 'read_at') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'thông báo % chỉ đổi read_at', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  IF OLD.read_at IS NOT NULL AND NEW.read_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.read_at THEN
    NEW.read_at := OLD.read_at;
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.org_cancel_deletion(p_correlation text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.org_cancel_deletion(p_correlation text)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_org uuid := app.current_org();
  v_actor uuid := nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '')::uuid;
  v_person uuid;
  o organizations%ROWTYPE;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO o FROM organizations x WHERE x.id = v_org FOR UPDATE;
  SELECT m.person_id INTO v_person FROM memberships m
   WHERE m.organization_id = v_org AND m.id = v_actor AND m.role = 'owner' AND m.status = 'active'
     AND m.valid_from <= now() AND (m.valid_to IS NULL OR m.valid_to > now());
  IF v_person IS NULL OR o.id IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'only an active owner may cancel organization deletion' USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'FORBIDDEN';
  END IF;
  IF o.status <> 'pending_deletion' THEN
    RETURN false;
  END IF;
  UPDATE organizations x
     SET status = 'active', deletion_requested_at = NULL, deletion_due_at = NULL, deletion_requested_by = NULL,
         version = x.version + 1
   WHERE x.id = v_org;
  INSERT INTO audit_events (id, organization_id, actor_type, actor_person_id, action, entity_type, entity_id, before, correlation_id)
  VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), v_org, 'user', v_person, 'organization.deletion_cancelled', 'organization', v_org,
          jsonb_build_object('due_at', o.deletion_due_at), coalesce(nullif(p_correlation, ''), 'org-deletion'));
  RETURN true;
END $function$
-- app.org_deletion_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.org_deletion_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(name text, labels jsonb, value double precision)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    SELECT 'hm_org_pending_deletion'::text, jsonb_build_object('state', s.state),
           (SELECT count(*) FROM organizations o
             WHERE o.status = 'pending_deletion'
               AND (o.deletion_due_at <= p_now) = (s.state = 'due'))::double precision
      FROM (VALUES ('waiting'), ('due')) AS s(state)
  $function$
-- app.org_purge_active(p_org uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.org_purge_active(p_org uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v text := current_setting('app.purge_org', true);
BEGIN
  IF p_org IS NULL OR v IS NULL OR v <> p_org::text THEN
    RETURN false;
  END IF;
  -- Executing role must own the purge function (i.e. we are inside the SECURITY DEFINER body); a caller-set GUC is not enough.
  IF current_user <> (SELECT pg_get_userbyid(p.proowner) FROM pg_proc p
                       WHERE p.oid = 'app.purge_organization(uuid, text[])'::regprocedure) THEN
    RETURN false;
  END IF;
  RETURN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM public.organizations o
                  WHERE o.id = p_org AND o.status = 'pending_deletion' AND o.deletion_due_at <= now());
END $function$
-- app.org_purge_due(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.org_purge_due(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, due_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    SELECT o.id, o.deletion_due_at FROM organizations o
     WHERE o.status = 'pending_deletion' AND o.deletion_due_at <= least(p_now, now())
     ORDER BY o.deletion_due_at, o.id
     LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 100)
  $function$
-- app.org_purge_file_keys(p_org uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.org_purge_file_keys(p_org uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(storage_key text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    SELECT f.storage_key FROM files f
      JOIN organizations o ON o.id = f.organization_id
     WHERE f.organization_id = p_org AND o.status = 'pending_deletion' AND o.deletion_due_at <= now()
     ORDER BY f.storage_key
  $function$
-- app.org_request_deletion(p_correlation text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.org_request_deletion(p_correlation text)
 RETURNS TABLE(status text, requested_at timestamp with time zone, due_at timestamp with time zone, created boolean)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_org uuid := app.current_org();
  v_actor uuid := nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '')::uuid;
  v_user uuid;
  v_person uuid;
  o organizations%ROWTYPE;
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO o FROM organizations x WHERE x.id = v_org FOR UPDATE;
  SELECT m.auth_user_id, m.person_id INTO v_user, v_person FROM memberships m
   WHERE m.organization_id = v_org AND m.id = v_actor AND m.role = 'owner' AND m.status = 'active'
     AND m.valid_from <= now() AND (m.valid_to IS NULL OR m.valid_to > now());
  IF v_user IS NULL OR o.id IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'only an active owner may request organization deletion' USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'FORBIDDEN';
  END IF;
  IF o.status = 'pending_deletion' THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT o.status, o.deletion_requested_at, o.deletion_due_at, false;
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  IF o.status <> 'active' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'organization % is %', v_org, o.status USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  UPDATE organizations x
     SET status = 'pending_deletion', deletion_requested_at = now(), deletion_due_at = now() + interval '30 days',
         deletion_requested_by = v_user, version = x.version + 1
   WHERE x.id = v_org
  RETURNING * INTO o;
  INSERT INTO audit_events (id, organization_id, actor_type, actor_person_id, action, entity_type, entity_id, after, correlation_id)
  VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), v_org, 'user', v_person, 'organization.deletion_requested', 'organization', v_org,
          jsonb_build_object('due_at', o.deletion_due_at), coalesce(nullif(p_correlation, ''), 'org-deletion'));
  RETURN QUERY SELECT o.status, o.deletion_requested_at, o.deletion_due_at, true;
END $function$
-- app.organizations_lifecycle_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.organizations_lifecycle_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF (NEW.status, NEW.deletion_requested_at, NEW.deletion_due_at, NEW.deletion_requested_by)
       IS DISTINCT FROM (OLD.status, OLD.deletion_requested_at, OLD.deletion_due_at, OLD.deletion_requested_by)
     AND current_user <> (SELECT pg_get_userbyid(c.relowner) FROM pg_class c WHERE c.oid = 'public.organizations'::regclass) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'organizations.status/deletion_* change only via app.org_request_deletion/app.org_cancel_deletion'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'FORBIDDEN';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.plan IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.plan
     AND current_user <> (SELECT pg_get_userbyid(c.relowner) FROM pg_class c WHERE c.oid = 'public.organizations'::regclass) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'organizations.plan change only by the table owner (docs/ops/runbooks/plan-change.md)'
      USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'FORBIDDEN';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.otp_sign_in_allowed(p_email text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.otp_sign_in_allowed(p_email text)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM auth."user" u
      WHERE u.email = lower(btrim(p_email))
        AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memberships m WHERE m.auth_user_id = u.id AND m.role = 'tenant' AND m.status = 'active'
                      AND m.valid_from <= now() AND (m.valid_to IS NULL OR m.valid_to > now()))
        AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM memberships m WHERE m.auth_user_id = u.id AND m.role <> 'tenant'
                          AND m.status IN ('invited','active','suspended')))
  $function$
-- app.outbox_claim(p_limit integer, p_per_org integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.outbox_claim(p_limit integer, p_per_org integer DEFAULT 10)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, organization_id uuid, event_type text, aggregate_id uuid, payload jsonb, correlation_id text, traceparent text)
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH due_orgs AS (
      SELECT o.organization_id FROM outbox o
      WHERE o.published_at IS NULL AND o.available_at <= now()
        AND (o.event_type = 'export.requested'
             OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = o.organization_id AND og.status = 'active'))
      GROUP BY o.organization_id
      ORDER BY min(o.created_at), o.organization_id
      LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 1000)),
    locked AS (
      SELECT x.id, x.organization_id, x.created_at FROM due_orgs d
      CROSS JOIN LATERAL (
        SELECT o.id, o.organization_id, o.created_at FROM outbox o
        WHERE o.organization_id = d.organization_id AND o.published_at IS NULL AND o.available_at <= now()
        AND (o.event_type = 'export.requested'
             OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = o.organization_id AND og.status = 'active'))
        ORDER BY o.created_at, o.id
        LIMIT least(greatest(p_per_org, 1), greatest(p_limit, 1), 1000)
        FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED) x),
    c AS (
      SELECT r.id, r.rn FROM (
        SELECT l.id, l.created_at,
               row_number() OVER (PARTITION BY l.organization_id ORDER BY l.created_at, l.id) AS rn
        FROM locked l) r
      ORDER BY r.rn, r.created_at, r.id
      LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 1000)),
    u AS (
      UPDATE outbox o SET available_at = now() + interval '60 seconds'
      FROM c WHERE o.id = c.id
      RETURNING o.id, o.organization_id, o.event_type, o.aggregate_id, o.payload, o.correlation_id, o.traceparent, o.created_at,
                c.rn)
    SELECT u.id, u.organization_id, u.event_type, u.aggregate_id, u.payload, u.correlation_id, u.traceparent
    FROM u ORDER BY u.rn, u.created_at, u.id
  $function$
-- app.outbox_mark(p_id uuid, p_error text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.outbox_mark(p_id uuid, p_error text)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    UPDATE outbox SET attempts = attempts + 1,
           published_at = CASE WHEN p_error IS NULL THEN now() ELSE published_at END,
           last_error = left(p_error, 500),
           available_at = CASE WHEN p_error IS NULL THEN available_at
                               ELSE now() + least(interval '5 minutes', make_interval(secs => 5 * power(2, least(attempts, 6)))) END
    WHERE id = p_id AND published_at IS NULL
  $function$
-- app.overdue_candidates(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.overdue_candidates(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(property_id uuid, property_name text, invoice_id uuid, payer_person_id uuid, open_amount bigint, due_on date, period_start date, business_date date, local_date date)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT i.property_id, p.name, i.id, i.payer_person_id, (i.total - i.allocated_total)::bigint, i.due_on, bp.period_start,
           i.business_date, (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date
    FROM invoices i
    JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = i.organization_id AND p.id = i.property_id
    LEFT JOIN notification_settings ns ON ns.organization_id = i.organization_id AND ns.property_id = i.property_id
    LEFT JOIN billing_periods bp ON bp.organization_id = i.organization_id AND bp.id = i.billing_period_id
    WHERE i.organization_id = p_org AND i.lease_id IS NOT NULL AND i.kind IN ('periodic','fee') AND i.due_on IS NOT NULL
      AND i.total > i.allocated_total
      AND ((p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date - i.due_on) = ANY (coalesce(ns.overdue_reminder_offsets_days, '{1,3,7}'))
      AND (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::time >= coalesce(ns.send_window_start, '08:00')
      AND (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::time < coalesce(ns.send_window_end, '20:00')
  $function$
-- app.payment_claim_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.payment_claim_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status <> 'submitted' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'báo chuyển % đã xử lý', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['status','matched_payment_id','handled_by','handled_at','reject_reason','updated_at','version'])
     IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['status','matched_payment_id','handled_by','handled_at','reject_reason','updated_at','version']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'báo chuyển %: không đổi nội dung đã báo', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.payment_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.payment_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
DECLARE
  fixed text[] := ARRAY['method','purpose','amount','currency','receiving_account_id','payer_person_id','deposit_id',
                        'shift_id','property_id','recorded_by','business_date','organization_id','id','created_at','source'];
  k text;
BEGIN
  FOREACH k IN ARRAY fixed LOOP
    IF (to_jsonb(NEW) -> k) IS DISTINCT FROM (to_jsonb(OLD) -> k) THEN
      RAISE EXCEPTION 'thanh toán %: không sửa % (INV-G4)', OLD.id, k USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  IF OLD.status = 'reversed'
     OR (OLD.status = 'confirmed' AND NEW.status NOT IN ('confirmed','reversed'))
     OR (OLD.status = 'pending' AND NEW.status = 'reversed')
     OR (OLD.status <> 'pending' AND (NEW.confirmed_by IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.confirmed_by
         OR NEW.evidence_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.evidence_id OR NEW.bank_transaction_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.bank_transaction_id
         OR NEW.receipt_no IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.receipt_no)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'thanh toán %: không thể chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status
      USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.payment_reservation_fixed() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.payment_reservation_fixed()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.reservation_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reservation_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'thanh toán %: không sửa reservation_id (INV-G4)', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.period_lock_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.period_lock_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.released_at IS NOT NULL
     OR NEW.released_at IS NULL
     OR (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['released_by','released_at','release_reason'])
        <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['released_by','released_at','release_reason']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'khoá kỳ %: chỉ mở khoá một lần, không sửa', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.plan_limits(p_plan text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.plan_limits(p_plan text)
 RETURNS TABLE(plan text, max_properties integer, max_units integer, max_active_members integer, max_storage_bytes bigint)
 LANGUAGE sql
 IMMUTABLE
 SET search_path TO 'pg_catalog', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT v.plan, v.max_properties, v.max_units, v.max_active_members, v.max_storage_bytes
    FROM (VALUES ('pilot'::text, 50, 20000, 200, 53687091200::bigint))
         AS v (plan, max_properties, max_units, max_active_members, max_storage_bytes)
    WHERE p_plan IS NULL OR v.plan = p_plan
    ORDER BY v.plan
  $function$
-- app.properties_due_period(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.properties_due_period(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_id uuid, property_id uuid, period_start date, period_end date)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT p.organization_id, p.id, m.s, (m.s + interval '1 month' - interval '1 day')::date
    FROM properties p
    CROSS JOIN LATERAL (SELECT date_trunc('month', (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone))::date AS s) m
    WHERE p.template IN ('rental','mixed') AND p.archived_at IS NULL AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = p.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM billing_periods b WHERE b.organization_id = p.organization_id AND b.property_id = p.id
                        AND b.period_start = m.s)
  $function$
-- app.property_staff(p_org uuid, p_property uuid, p_roles text[]) owner=<admin>

 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT DISTINCT m.person_id FROM memberships m
    WHERE m.organization_id = p_org AND m.status = 'active' AND m.role = ANY (p_roles)
      AND (m.all_properties OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM property_scopes ps WHERE ps.organization_id = m.organization_id
                                         AND ps.membership_id = m.id AND ps.property_id = p_property))
  $function$

 LANGUAGE sql

           OR (coalesce(app.actor_role() NOT IN ('tenant','guest'), false) AND app.actor_can_see_property(p))
  $function$
-- app.purge_auth_sessions(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.purge_auth_sessions(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    WITH due AS (
      SELECT s.id FROM auth."session" s
      WHERE s."expiresAt" < least(p_now, now()) - make_interval(days => greatest(p_days, 1))
      ORDER BY s."expiresAt", s.id
      LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 5000)
      FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    ), x AS (
      DELETE FROM auth."session" s USING due WHERE s.id = due.id RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM x
  $function$

-- app.purge_import_rows(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.purge_import_rows(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH due AS (
      SELECT b.organization_id, b.id FROM import_batches b
      JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = b.organization_id AND p.id = b.property_id
      WHERE b.status IN ('committed','cancelled')
        AND (coalesce(b.committed_at, b.cancelled_at) AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date + p_days <= (p_now AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date
    ), x AS (
      UPDATE import_rows r SET raw = '{}'::jsonb, data = NULL
      FROM due WHERE r.organization_id = due.organization_id AND r.batch_id = due.id
        AND (r.raw <> '{}'::jsonb OR r.data IS NOT NULL)
      RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM x
  $function$
-- app.purge_notification_deliveries(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_done_days integer, p_failed_days integer, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.purge_notification_deliveries(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_done_days integer, p_failed_days integer, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH due AS (
      SELECT d.id FROM notification_deliveries d
      WHERE (d.status IN ('sent','suppressed')
             AND d.updated_at < least(p_now, now()) - make_interval(days => greatest(p_done_days, 1)))
         OR (d.status IN ('failed','dead')
             AND d.updated_at < least(p_now, now()) - make_interval(days => greatest(p_failed_days, 1)))
      ORDER BY d.updated_at, d.id
      LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 5000)
      FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    ), x AS (
      DELETE FROM notification_deliveries d USING due WHERE d.id = due.id RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM x
  $function$
-- app.purge_notifications(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.purge_notifications(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_days integer, p_limit integer)

      SELECT n.id FROM notifications n

      ORDER BY n.created_at, n.id
      LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 5000)
      FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED
    ), x AS (
      DELETE FROM notifications n USING due WHERE n.id = due.id RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM x
  $function$
-- app.purge_organization(p_org uuid, p_deleted_keys text[]) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.purge_organization(p_org uuid, p_deleted_keys text[])
 RETURNS jsonb
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  v_order text[] := ARRAY['audit_events', 'blocks', 'email_budget_days', 'exceptions', 'export_jobs', 'guest_access_links', 'idempotency_keys', 'identity_documents', 'import_external_refs', 'import_rows', 'invitations', 'journal_lines', 'lease_occupants', 'lease_settlements', 'meter_events', 'meter_readings', 'notification_deliveries', 'notification_preferences', 'notification_settings', 'outbox', 'payment_allocations', 'payment_claims', 'period_locks', 'product_events', 'property_scopes', 'rate_plans', 'reconciliation_breaks', 'residence_notices', 'service_prices', 'shift_discrepancies', 'tasks', 'work_orders', 'handovers', 'import_batches', 'invoice_lines', 'journal_entries', 'meters', 'notifications', 'payments', 'charges', 'deposits', 'files', 'inbox+bank_transactions', 'invoices', 'shifts', 'billing_periods', 'lease_versions', 'receiving_accounts', 'services', 'stays', 'approval_requests', 'leases', 'reservation_items', 'allocations', 'reservations', 'memberships', 'units', 'floors', 'persons', 'room_types', 'properties'];
  v_org organizations%ROWTYPE;
  v_t text;
  n bigint;
  n2 bigint;
  v_counts jsonb := '{}'::jsonb;
  v_total bigint := 0;
  v_objects integer;
  v_now timestamptz := now();
  v_digest text;
  v_id uuid := gen_random_uuid();
BEGIN
  SELECT * INTO v_org FROM organizations o WHERE o.id = p_org FOR UPDATE;
  IF NOT FOUND OR v_org.status <> 'pending_deletion' OR v_org.deletion_due_at > now() THEN
    RETURN NULL;
  END IF;
  -- Objects are deleted by the worker BEFORE this call; a file row it did not delete (written after it listed keys) ⇒ skip, retry.
  IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM files f
              WHERE f.organization_id = p_org AND NOT (f.storage_key = ANY (coalesce(p_deleted_keys, '{}'::text[])))) THEN
    RETURN jsonb_build_object('skipped', 'new_files');
  END IF;
  SELECT count(*)::int INTO v_objects FROM files f WHERE f.organization_id = p_org;
  PERFORM set_config('app.org_id', p_org::text, true);
  PERFORM set_config('app.purge_org', p_org::text, true);
  -- Deferred last-owner check must fire HERE (definer rights, bypass active), not at COMMIT as app_worker.
  SET CONSTRAINTS trg_memberships_last_owner IMMEDIATE;
  FOREACH v_t IN ARRAY v_order LOOP
    IF v_t = 'inbox+bank_transactions' THEN
      -- RESTRICT FK cycle: both DELETEs in ONE statement; RI checks run at statement end when both sides are gone.
      WITH a AS (DELETE FROM inbox x WHERE x.organization_id = p_org RETURNING 1),
           b AS (DELETE FROM bank_transactions x WHERE x.organization_id = p_org RETURNING 1)
      SELECT (SELECT count(*) FROM a), (SELECT count(*) FROM b) INTO n, n2;
      v_counts := v_counts || jsonb_build_object('inbox', n, 'bank_transactions', n2);
      v_total := v_total + n + n2;
    ELSE
      EXECUTE format('DELETE FROM %I WHERE organization_id = $1', v_t) USING p_org;
      GET DIAGNOSTICS n = ROW_COUNT;
      v_counts := v_counts || jsonb_build_object(v_t, n);
      v_total := v_total + n;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  DELETE FROM organizations o WHERE o.id = p_org;
  v_counts := v_counts || jsonb_build_object('organizations', 1);
  v_total := v_total + 1;
  PERFORM set_config('app.purge_org', '', true);
  v_digest := encode(sha256(convert_to(
    p_org::text || '|' || to_char(v_now, 'YYYY-MM-DD"T"HH24:MI:SS.US"Z"') || '|' || v_counts::text, 'UTF8')), 'hex');
  INSERT INTO org_deletion_certificates
    (id, deleted_organization_id, requested_by, requested_at, due_at, purged_at, counts, total_rows, objects_deleted, digest)
  VALUES (v_id, p_org, v_org.deletion_requested_by, v_org.deletion_requested_at, v_org.deletion_due_at, v_now, v_counts,
          v_total, v_objects, v_digest);
  RETURN jsonb_build_object('certificate_id', v_id, 'total_rows', v_total, 'objects_deleted', v_objects,
                            'counts', v_counts, 'digest', v_digest);
END $function$

  $function$

-- app.rate_limit_hit(p_scope text, p_subject text, p_limit integer, p_window_seconds integer, p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.rate_limit_hit(p_scope text, p_subject text, p_limit integer, p_window_seconds integer, p_now timestamp with time zone DEFAULT now())
 RETURNS TABLE(allowed boolean, used integer, retry_after integer)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE
  w_secs integer := least(greatest(p_window_seconds, 1), 86400);
  w_start timestamptz := to_timestamp(floor(extract(epoch FROM p_now) / w_secs) * w_secs);
  n integer;
BEGIN
  IF p_limit IS NULL OR p_limit < 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'rate_limit_hit: p_limit phải >= 1' USING ERRCODE = '22023';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtextextended('rate_limit:' || p_scope || ':' || p_subject, 0));
  SELECT c.hits INTO n FROM rate_limit_counters c
   WHERE c.scope = p_scope AND c.subject = p_subject AND c.window_start = w_start;
  n := coalesce(n, 0);
  IF n >= p_limit THEN
    RETURN QUERY SELECT false, n,
      greatest(1, ceil(extract(epoch FROM w_start + w_secs * interval '1 second' - p_now))::integer);
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  INSERT INTO rate_limit_counters AS c (scope, subject, window_start, hits) VALUES (p_scope, p_subject, w_start, 1)
  ON CONFLICT (scope, subject, window_start) DO UPDATE SET hits = c.hits + 1
  RETURNING c.hits INTO n;
  DELETE FROM rate_limit_counters d WHERE d.ctid IN (
    SELECT x.ctid FROM rate_limit_counters x WHERE x.window_start < p_now - interval '1 day' LIMIT 50
    FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED);
  RETURN QUERY SELECT true, n, 0;
END $function$

  $function$
-- app.reconciliation_break_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.reconciliation_break_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status <> 'open' OR NEW.status <> 'resolved'
     OR (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['status','resolution','resolved_by','resolved_at','resolved_payment_id','reason','detail',
                               'updated_at','version'])
        <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['status','resolution','resolved_by','resolved_at','resolved_payment_id','reason','detail',
                                  'updated_at','version'])
     OR (NEW.reason <> OLD.reason AND NEW.reason <> 'THIRD_PARTY_PAYER') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'break %: chỉ đóng một lần, có lý do', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.reconciliation_breaks_overdue(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_sla_hours integer, p_limit integer) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.reconciliation_breaks_overdue(p_now timestamp with time zone, p_sla_hours integer, p_limit integer)
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_id uuid, property_id uuid, break_id uuid, assignee uuid, reason text, age_hours integer)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT b.organization_id, b.property_id, b.id, b.assignee, b.reason,
           floor(extract(epoch FROM p_now - b.created_at) / extract(epoch FROM interval '1 hour'))::integer
    FROM reconciliation_breaks b
    WHERE b.status = 'open' AND b.created_at <= p_now - make_interval(hours => greatest(p_sla_hours, 0)) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = b.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
    ORDER BY b.created_at, b.id
    LIMIT least(greatest(p_limit, 1), 5000)
  $function$
-- app.reconciliation_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.reconciliation_metrics(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(name text, labels jsonb, value double precision)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_reconciliation_breaks_open'::text, '{}'::jsonb, count(*)::double precision
    FROM reconciliation_breaks WHERE status = 'open';
  RETURN QUERY SELECT 'hm_reconciliation_breaks_oldest_open_age_seconds'::text, '{}'::jsonb,
    coalesce(max(extract(epoch FROM p_now - created_at)), 0)::double precision
    FROM reconciliation_breaks WHERE status = 'open';
END $function$

  $function$
-- app.reservation_transition() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.reservation_transition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status AND NOT (
       (OLD.status = 'draft' AND NEW.status IN ('held','cancelled'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'held' AND NEW.status IN ('confirmed','expired','cancelled'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'confirmed' AND NEW.status IN ('in_house','cancelled','no_show'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'in_house' AND NEW.status = 'checked_out')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking %: không chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.booker_person_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.booker_person_id OR NEW.source IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.source
     OR NEW.number IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.number OR NEW.property_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.property_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'booking %: không sửa người đặt/nguồn/số', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.residence_notice_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.residence_notice_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status = 'submitted' OR (OLD.status = 'exported' AND NEW.status = 'pending')
     OR NEW.stay_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.stay_id OR NEW.is_foreign IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.is_foreign THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'thông báo lưu trú %: không lùi trạng thái/không sửa sau khi đã thông báo', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.resolve_guest_link(p_hash bytea) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.resolve_guest_link(p_hash bytea)
 RETURNS TABLE(id uuid, organization_id uuid, property_id uuid, stay_id uuid, reservation_id uuid, expires_at timestamp with time zone, revoked_at timestamp with time zone, locked_at timestamp with time zone, failed_attempts integer, phone_last4 text, second_factor boolean)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT g.id, g.organization_id, g.property_id, coalesce(g.stay_id, s.id), coalesce(g.reservation_id, ri.reservation_id),
           g.expires_at, g.revoked_at, g.locked_at, g.failed_attempts,
           right(regexp_replace(coalesce(bp.phone, ''), '[^0-9]', '', 'g'), 4),
           coalesce((p.settings ->> 'guest_link_second_factor')::boolean, true)
    FROM guest_access_links g
    JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = g.organization_id AND p.id = g.property_id
    LEFT JOIN stays s0 ON s0.organization_id = g.organization_id AND s0.id = g.stay_id
    LEFT JOIN reservation_items ri ON ri.organization_id = g.organization_id
         AND (ri.id = s0.reservation_item_id OR (g.reservation_id IS NOT NULL AND ri.reservation_id = g.reservation_id))
    LEFT JOIN stays s ON s.organization_id = ri.organization_id AND s.reservation_item_id = ri.id
    LEFT JOIN reservations r ON r.organization_id = ri.organization_id AND r.id = ri.reservation_id
    LEFT JOIN persons bp ON bp.organization_id = r.organization_id AND bp.id = r.booker_person_id
    WHERE g.token_hash = p_hash AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = g.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
    LIMIT 1
  $function$
-- app.retention_person_activity(p_org uuid, p_person uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.retention_person_activity(p_org uuid, p_person uuid)
 RETURNS TABLE(has_open boolean, stay_lease_end timestamp with time zone, relation_end timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    WITH ev(kind, is_open, at) AS (
      SELECT 'stay', s.status IN ('expected','in_house'),
             greatest(s.created_at, s.updated_at, s.checked_out_at, (s.debt_due_date + 1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')
        FROM stays s
       WHERE s.organization_id = p_org
         AND (s.primary_guest_person_id = p_person OR s.debtor_person_id = p_person
              OR s.additional_guest_person_ids @> ARRAY[p_person])
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'stay', r.status IN ('draft','held','confirmed','in_house'), greatest(r.created_at, r.updated_at, r.cancelled_at)
        FROM reservations r WHERE r.organization_id = p_org AND r.booker_person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'stay', l.status NOT IN ('closed','cancelled'),
             greatest(l.created_at, l.updated_at, (l.moved_out_on + 1)::timestamp AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')
        FROM leases l
       WHERE l.organization_id = p_org
         AND (l.representative_person_id = p_person
              OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM lease_occupants o
                         WHERE o.organization_id = p_org AND o.lease_id = l.id AND o.person_id = p_person))
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'money', i.open_amount <> 0, greatest(i.issued_at, i.updated_at)
        FROM invoices i WHERE i.organization_id = p_org AND i.payer_person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      -- payments: pending, or confirmed receivable with an unallocated part while the payer still has CUSTOMER_CREDIT > 0 here.
      SELECT 'money', p.status = 'pending'
               OR (p.status = 'confirmed' AND p.purpose = 'receivable' AND p.amount > p.allocated_total
                   AND coalesce((SELECT sum(j.credit - j.debit) FROM journal_lines j
                                 WHERE j.organization_id = p_org AND j.property_id = p.property_id
                                   AND j.account = 'CUSTOMER_CREDIT' AND j.person_id = p_person), 0) > 0),
             greatest(p.created_at, p.updated_at, p.confirmed_at)
        FROM payments p WHERE p.organization_id = p_org AND p.payer_person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'money', d.balance <> 0, greatest(d.created_at, d.updated_at)
        FROM deposits d WHERE d.organization_id = p_org AND d.payer_person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'money', false, j.created_at
        FROM journal_lines j WHERE j.organization_id = p_org AND j.person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      -- CUSTOMER_CREDIT balance per property <> 0: money still owed to/by the customer.
      SELECT 'money', sum(j.credit - j.debit) <> 0, max(j.created_at)
        FROM journal_lines j
       WHERE j.organization_id = p_org AND j.account = 'CUSTOMER_CREDIT' AND j.person_id = p_person
       GROUP BY j.property_id
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'money', c.status = 'submitted', greatest(c.created_at, c.updated_at)
        FROM payment_claims c WHERE c.organization_id = p_org AND c.person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'other', m.status IN ('invited','active','suspended'), greatest(m.created_at, m.updated_at, m.revoked_at, m.valid_to)
        FROM memberships m WHERE m.organization_id = p_org AND m.person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'other', v.status = 'pending', greatest(v.created_at, v.updated_at, v.expires_at)
        FROM invitations v WHERE v.organization_id = p_org AND v.person_id = p_person
      UNION ALL
      SELECT 'other', w.status IN ('open','assigned','in_progress'), greatest(w.created_at, w.updated_at)
        FROM work_orders w WHERE w.organization_id = p_org AND w.reporter_person_id = p_person
    )
    SELECT coalesce(bool_or(ev.is_open), false), max(ev.at) FILTER (WHERE ev.kind = 'stay'), max(ev.at) FROM ev
  $function$
-- app.select_property(p_property uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.select_property(p_property uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF p_property IS NOT NULL AND NOT app.actor_can_see_property(p_property) THEN RETURN false; END IF;
  UPDATE memberships SET selected_property_id = p_property
   WHERE id = nullif(current_setting('app.actor_id', true), '')::uuid AND organization_id = app.current_org()
     AND status = 'active' AND selected_property_id IS DISTINCT FROM p_property;
  RETURN true;
END $function$
-- app.session_guard_erased() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.session_guard_erased()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
BEGIN
  -- Locking read: waits for an in-flight dsar_erase_account (FOR UPDATE on the user), then re-checks the committed row.
  PERFORM 1 FROM auth."user" u
   WHERE u.id = NEW."userId" AND u.email !~ '^deleted\+[0-9a-f-]{36}@deleted\.invalid$'
   FOR KEY SHARE;
  IF NOT FOUND THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'account deleted' USING ERRCODE = '42501', HINT = 'ACCOUNT_DELETED';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.set_updated_at() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.set_updated_at()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  NEW.updated_at := now();
  NEW.version := OLD.version + 1;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.shift_discrepancy_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.shift_discrepancy_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status = 'resolved'
     OR (to_jsonb(NEW) - ARRAY['responsible_membership_id','status','resolved_by','resolved_at','adjustment_entry_id',
                               'resolution_note','updated_at','version'])
        <> (to_jsonb(OLD) - ARRAY['responsible_membership_id','status','resolved_by','resolved_at','adjustment_entry_id',
                                  'resolution_note','updated_at','version']) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'chênh lệch ca %: không sửa số tiền/ca; đã xử lý thì bất biến', OLD.id
      USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.shift_guard() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.shift_guard()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status = 'handed_over'
     OR (OLD.status = 'closed' AND (NEW.status NOT IN ('closed','handed_over')
         OR NEW.opening_cash <> OLD.opening_cash OR NEW.counted_cash IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.counted_cash
         OR NEW.expected_cash IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.expected_cash OR NEW.closed_by IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.closed_by
         OR NEW.closed_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.closed_at OR NEW.opened_by <> OLD.opened_by))
     OR (OLD.status = 'open' AND (NEW.opening_cash <> OLD.opening_cash OR NEW.opened_by <> OLD.opened_by)) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'ca % đã chốt — không sửa được (INV-F6)', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.stay_notice_candidates(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.stay_notice_candidates(p_org uuid, p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(property_id uuid, property_name text, notice_id uuid, unit_code text, due_at timestamp with time zone, due_at_local text, level text, business_date date)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT rn.property_id, p.name, rn.id, u.code, rn.due_at,
           to_char(rn.due_at AT TIME ZONE p.timezone, 'HH24:MI DD/MM/YYYY'),
           CASE WHEN p_now >= rn.due_at THEN 'overdue'
                WHEN p_now >= rn.due_at - interval '30 minutes' THEN 'urgent' ELSE 'normal' END,
           app.business_date(p_org, rn.property_id, p_now)
    FROM residence_notices rn
    JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = rn.organization_id AND p.id = rn.property_id
    JOIN stays s ON s.organization_id = rn.organization_id AND s.id = rn.stay_id
    JOIN units u ON u.organization_id = s.organization_id AND u.id = s.unit_id
    WHERE rn.organization_id = p_org AND rn.status IN ('pending','exported') AND rn.due_at IS NOT NULL
      AND s.status IN ('in_house','checked_out')
    UNION ALL
    SELECT p.id, p.name, NULL::uuid, NULL::text, NULL::timestamptz, NULL::text, 'config_missing',
           app.business_date(p_org, p.id, p_now)
    FROM properties p
    LEFT JOIN notification_settings ns ON ns.organization_id = p.organization_id AND ns.property_id = p.id
    WHERE p.organization_id = p_org AND ns.stay_notice_deadline IS NULL
      AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM residence_notices rn WHERE rn.organization_id = p.organization_id AND rn.property_id = p.id
                    AND rn.status = 'pending' AND rn.due_at IS NULL)
  $function$
-- app.stay_transition() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.stay_transition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status AND NOT (
       (OLD.status = 'expected' AND NEW.status IN ('in_house','cancelled','no_show'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'in_house' AND NEW.status = 'checked_out')) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lượt ở %: không chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  -- Lượt ở đã kết thúc bất biến (dấu vết lưu trú).
  IF OLD.status IN ('checked_out','cancelled','no_show') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'lượt ở % đã kết thúc', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.stays_overdue(p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.stays_overdue(p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_id uuid, property_id uuid, stay_id uuid, allocation_id uuid, related_allocation_id uuid)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT s.organization_id, s.property_id, s.id, a.id,
           (SELECT o.id FROM allocations o WHERE o.organization_id = a.organization_id AND o.unit_id = a.unit_id AND o.active AND o.kind IN ('stay', 'lease')
              AND o.id <> a.id AND lower(o.during) >= upper(a.during) ORDER BY lower(o.during), o.id LIMIT 1)
    FROM stays s
    JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = s.organization_id AND p.id = s.property_id
    JOIN allocations a ON a.organization_id = s.organization_id AND a.source_type = 'stay' AND a.source_id = s.id
                      AND a.active AND a.kind = 'stay'
    WHERE s.status = 'in_house' AND NOT upper_inf(a.during) AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM organizations og WHERE og.id = s.organization_id AND og.status = 'active')
      AND upper(a.during) + make_interval(mins => p.overstay_grace_minutes) <= p_now
      AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM allocations l WHERE l.organization_id = a.organization_id AND l.source_type = 'stay'
                        AND l.source_id = a.source_id AND l.active AND l.kind = 'stay' AND l.id <> a.id
                        AND lower(l.during) >= upper(a.during))
  $function$
-- app.task_transition() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.task_transition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status IN ('verified','cancelled') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'việc % đã kết thúc', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status AND NOT (
       (OLD.status = 'open' AND NEW.status IN ('assigned','cancelled'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'assigned' AND NEW.status IN ('in_progress','cancelled','assigned'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'in_progress' AND NEW.status IN ('done','cancelled'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'done' AND NEW.status IN ('verified','assigned'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'done' AND NEW.status = 'cancelled' AND OLD.kind = 'cleaning' AND EXISTS (
          SELECT 1 FROM tasks t WHERE t.organization_id = OLD.organization_id AND t.unit_id = OLD.unit_id AND t.kind = 'cleaning'
            AND t.id <> OLD.id AND t.status IN ('open','assigned','in_progress')))) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'việc %: không chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.unit_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.unit_id OR NEW.kind IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.kind THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'việc %: không đổi phòng/loại việc', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.tenant_access_on_lease_close() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.tenant_access_on_lease_close()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE days integer;
BEGIN
  IF NEW.status = 'closed' AND OLD.status IS DISTINCT FROM 'closed' THEN
    SELECT coalesce((o.settings ->> 'tenant_readonly_days')::int, 30) INTO days FROM organizations o WHERE o.id = NEW.organization_id;
    UPDATE memberships m SET valid_to = now() + make_interval(days => greatest(days, 0)), version = m.version + 1
     WHERE m.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND m.person_id = NEW.representative_person_id AND m.role = 'tenant'
       AND m.status = 'active' AND (m.valid_to IS NULL OR m.valid_to > now() + make_interval(days => greatest(days, 0)))
       AND NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM leases l WHERE l.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND l.id <> NEW.id
                         AND l.representative_person_id = NEW.representative_person_id
                         AND l.status IN ('signed_awaiting_handover','active','notice_given','settling'));
  ELSIF NEW.status IN ('signed_awaiting_handover','active','notice_given','settling')
        AND OLD.status NOT IN ('signed_awaiting_handover','active','notice_given','settling') THEN
    -- Hợp đồng mới có hiệu lực ⇒ bỏ hạn chỉ-đọc đã đặt khi hợp đồng cũ đóng (review lát 4); quyền đã thu hồi giữ nguyên.
    UPDATE memberships m SET valid_to = NULL, version = m.version + 1
     WHERE m.organization_id = NEW.organization_id AND m.person_id = NEW.representative_person_id AND m.role = 'tenant'
       AND m.status = 'active' AND m.valid_to IS NOT NULL;
  END IF;
  RETURN NULL;
END $function$
-- app.tenant_meters() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.tenant_meters()
 RETURNS TABLE(meter_id uuid, since date, until date)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT m.id, l.billing_start_on,
           CASE WHEN l.status IN ('settling','closed') THEN coalesce(l.moved_out_on, l.billing_start_on - 1) END
    FROM meters m
    JOIN leases l ON l.organization_id = m.organization_id AND l.unit_id = m.unit_id
    WHERE m.organization_id = app.current_org() AND app.actor_role() = 'tenant'
      AND l.representative_person_id = app.actor_person_id()
      AND l.status IN ('signed_awaiting_handover','active','notice_given','settling','closed')
  $function$
-- app.tenant_property_ids() owner=<admin>

 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT DISTINCT l.property_id FROM leases l
    WHERE l.organization_id = app.current_org() AND app.actor_role() = 'tenant'
      AND l.representative_person_id = app.actor_person_id()
      AND l.status IN ('signed_awaiting_handover','active','notice_given','settling','closed')
  $function$
-- app.tenant_rents_unit(p_unit uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.tenant_rents_unit(p_unit uuid)
 RETURNS boolean
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT app.actor_role() = 'tenant' AND app.actor_person_id() IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (
      SELECT 1 FROM leases l JOIN properties p ON p.organization_id = l.organization_id AND p.id = l.property_id
      WHERE l.organization_id = app.current_org() AND l.unit_id = p_unit AND l.status IN ('active','notice_given')
        AND (l.representative_person_id = app.actor_person_id()
             OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM lease_occupants o
                        WHERE o.organization_id = l.organization_id AND o.lease_id = l.id AND o.person_id = app.actor_person_id()
                          AND o.role IN ('primary','co_tenant')
                          AND (o.to_on IS NULL OR o.to_on >= (now() AT TIME ZONE p.timezone)::date))))
  $function$
-- app.unit_arrivals(p_property uuid, p_date date) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.unit_arrivals(p_property uuid, p_date date)
 RETURNS TABLE(unit_id uuid, arrival_at timestamp with time zone)
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT s.unit_id, min(lower(a.during))
    FROM stays s
    JOIN reservation_items i ON i.organization_id = s.organization_id AND i.id = s.reservation_item_id
    JOIN allocations a ON a.organization_id = i.organization_id AND a.id = i.allocation_id AND a.active
    WHERE s.organization_id = app.current_org() AND s.property_id = p_property AND s.status = 'expected'
      AND i.arrival_date = p_date AND app.actor_can_see_property(p_property)
    GROUP BY s.unit_id
  $function$
-- app.unlink_product_events_actor(p_user_id uuid) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.unlink_product_events_actor(p_user_id uuid)
 RETURNS integer
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
 SET "TimeZone" TO 'UTC'
AS $function$
    WITH x AS (
      UPDATE product_events e SET actor_user_id = NULL
      WHERE p_user_id IS NOT NULL AND e.actor_user_id = p_user_id
      RETURNING 1)
    SELECT count(*)::int FROM x
  $function$
-- app.unsubscribe_email(p_org uuid, p_person uuid, p_type text, p_correlation text) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.unsubscribe_email(p_org uuid, p_person uuid, p_type text, p_correlation text)
 RETURNS TABLE(organization_name text, changed boolean)
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
DECLARE prev text;
BEGIN
  IF p_type = 'account_security' THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'loại bắt buộc' USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'PREFERENCE_REQUIRED';
  END IF;
  IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM persons WHERE organization_id = p_org AND id = p_person) THEN
    RETURN;
  END IF;
  SELECT state INTO prev FROM notification_preferences
   WHERE organization_id = p_org AND person_id = p_person AND type = p_type AND channel = 'email' FOR UPDATE;
  INSERT INTO notification_preferences (id, organization_id, person_id, type, channel, state, source)
  VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), p_org, p_person, p_type, 'email', 'opted_out', 'email_link')
  ON CONFLICT (organization_id, person_id, type, channel)
    DO UPDATE SET state = 'opted_out', source = 'email_link', changed_at = now()
    WHERE notification_preferences.state <> 'opted_out';
  IF prev IS DISTINCT FROM 'opted_out' THEN
    INSERT INTO audit_events (id, organization_id, actor_type, actor_person_id, action, entity_type, entity_id, before, after,
                              correlation_id)
    VALUES (gen_random_uuid(), p_org, 'user', p_person, 'notification.preference_changed', 'person', p_person,
            jsonb_build_object('type', p_type, 'channel', 'email', 'state', coalesce(prev, 'opted_in')),
            jsonb_build_object('type', p_type, 'channel', 'email', 'state', 'opted_out', 'source', 'email_link'),
            p_correlation);
  END IF;
  RETURN QUERY SELECT o.name, prev IS DISTINCT FROM 'opted_out' FROM organizations o WHERE o.id = p_org;
END $function$
-- app.visible_property_ids() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.visible_property_ids()
 RETURNS SETOF uuid
 LANGUAGE sql
 STABLE SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    SELECT p.id FROM properties p
    WHERE p.organization_id = app.current_org()
      AND CASE
        WHEN app.actor_role() IN ('tenant','guest') THEN
          app.actor_role() = 'tenant' AND app.actor_person_id() IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (
            SELECT 1 FROM leases l
            WHERE l.organization_id = p.organization_id AND l.property_id = p.id
              AND l.status IN ('pending_signature','signed_awaiting_handover','active','notice_given','settling','closed')
              AND (l.representative_person_id = app.actor_person_id()
                   OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM lease_occupants o
                              WHERE o.organization_id = l.organization_id AND o.lease_id = l.id
                                AND o.person_id = app.actor_person_id() AND o.role IN ('primary','co_tenant'))))
        ELSE app.actor_can_see_property(p.id)
      END
  $function$
-- app.work_order_transition() owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.work_order_transition()
 RETURNS trigger
 LANGUAGE plpgsql
AS $function$
BEGIN
  IF OLD.status IN ('verified','cancelled') THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiếu % đã kết thúc', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.status IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.status AND NOT (
       (OLD.status = 'open' AND NEW.status IN ('assigned','cancelled'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'assigned' AND NEW.status IN ('in_progress','cancelled','assigned'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'in_progress' AND NEW.status IN ('done','cancelled'))
    OR (OLD.status = 'done' AND NEW.status IN ('verified','assigned'))) THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiếu %: không chuyển % → %', OLD.id, OLD.status, NEW.status USING ERRCODE = '23514', HINT = 'INVALID_TRANSITION';
  END IF;
  IF NEW.unit_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.unit_id OR NEW.source IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.source
     OR NEW.reporter_membership_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reporter_membership_id
     OR NEW.reporter_person_id IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.reporter_person_id THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'phiếu %: không đổi phòng/người báo', OLD.id USING ERRCODE = '55000', HINT = 'DOCUMENT_IMMUTABLE';
  END IF;
  RETURN NEW;
END $function$
-- app.worker_heartbeat(p_name text, p_now timestamp with time zone) owner=<admin>
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION app.worker_heartbeat(p_name text, p_now timestamp with time zone)
 RETURNS void
 LANGUAGE sql
 SECURITY DEFINER
 SET search_path TO 'public', 'pg_temp'
AS $function$
    INSERT INTO worker_heartbeats (name, beat_at, started_at) VALUES (p_name, p_now, p_now)
    ON CONFLICT (name) DO UPDATE SET beat_at = excluded.beat_at
  $function$
